Path: .github/workflows/ci.yml
Git mode: 100644
Byte size: 3009
SHA-256: 522925be556b81659cc4aeadecaa71adeda47f7781380848ca5354c076720a9f

--- CONTENT START ---
name: CI
on: [push, pull_request, workflow_dispatch]
env:
  CARGO_TERM_COLOR: always
jobs:
  standalone_regression:
    # These tests remain runnable while the checked-in workspace is missing modules.
    runs-on: ubuntu-latest
    steps:
      - uses: actions/checkout@v4
      - uses: dtolnay/rust-toolchain@stable
        with:
          components: rustfmt
      - name: Check formatting of touched Rust files
        run: |
          rustfmt --check --edition 2021 crates/codec-cli/src/digest.rs crates/codec-cli/src/main.rs crates/codec-cli/tests/integration_test.rs crates/codec-core/src/time.rs verification/time_standalone.rs
      - name: Verify RFC 1321 MD5 test vectors without Cargo workspace metadata
        run: |
          rustc --edition 2021 --test crates/codec-cli/src/digest.rs -o /tmp/codec-md5-tests
          /tmp/codec-md5-tests
      - name: Verify clock synchronizer tests independently of workspace metadata
        run: |
          rustc --edition 2021 --test verification/time_standalone.rs -o /tmp/codec-time-tests
          /tmp/codec-time-tests
  source_inventory:
    runs-on: ubuntu-latest
    steps:
      - uses: actions/checkout@v4
      - name: Check that all referenced crate, module and fuzz sources are committed
        run: python3 verification/source_inventory.py

  build_and_test:
    needs: source_inventory
    runs-on: ${{ matrix.os }}
    strategy:
      fail-fast: false
      matrix:
        os: [ubuntu-latest, windows-latest, macos-latest]
    steps:
      - uses: actions/checkout@v4
      - uses: dtolnay/rust-toolchain@stable
        with:
          components: rustfmt, clippy
      - uses: ilammy/msvc-dev-cmd@v1
        if: runner.os == 'Windows'
      - run: cargo fmt --all -- --check
      - run: cargo clippy --locked --workspace --all-targets -- -D warnings
      - run: cargo test --locked --workspace
      - run: cargo build --locked --workspace --release
      - run: cargo test --locked --workspace --release
      - name: Scalar Differential Path Check (CODEC_SIMD_OFF=all)
        env:
          CODEC_SIMD_OFF: all
        run: cargo test --locked --workspace
  tsan_concurrency:
    needs: source_inventory
    runs-on: ubuntu-latest
    steps:
      - uses: actions/checkout@v4
      - uses: dtolnay/rust-toolchain@nightly
        with:
          components: rust-src
      - name: ThreadSanitizer Wave-Front and DualRef VBI Verification
        env:
          RUSTFLAGS: -Z sanitizer=thread
        run: cargo +nightly test --locked -p codec-core -p codec-memory -p codec-av1 --target x86_64-unknown-linux-gnu
  fuzz_smoke:
    needs: source_inventory
    runs-on: ubuntu-latest
    steps:
      - uses: actions/checkout@v4
      - uses: dtolnay/rust-toolchain@nightly
      - run: cargo install cargo-fuzz --locked
      - run: cargo fuzz run mp4 -- -max_total_time=30 -max_len=65536
      - run: cargo fuzz run av1 -- -max_total_time=30 -max_len=65536
      - run: cargo fuzz run av1_settings -- -max_total_time=30 -max_len=65536
--- CONTENT END ---

# Advanced Rust Multimedia Codec and Intelligent Playback Library

Memory-safe Rust workspace combining a normative AV1 decoding engine, ISOBMFF/fMP4
demuxer, zero-copy Vulkan Video (`VK_KHR_video_decode_av1`) hardware offload,
closed-loop PID audio-video synchronization, a declarative shader/filter-graph
domain-specific language (`codec-dsl`), and real-time neural optical-flow frame
interpolation (`codec-neural`).

## Current source-tree status

**This checkout is incomplete and does not currently pass Cargo builds or the full CI suite.**
The workspace refers to absent `codec-dsl` and `codec-neural` crates;
`codec-core` refers to missing `hwaccel`, `simd`, and `threading` modules;
and the AV1 crate is also missing advertised inter/intra/partition/transform and
Vulkan modules. The `av1_settings` fuzz target source is absent as well.
Restore the actual implementations from the development worktree or an earlier
revision rather than replacing them with no-op placeholders. The architecture
table below describes the intended design, **not the capabilities verified in
this checkout**.

The CLI's `--md5` option computes the real MD5 of each **compressed AV1 sample**
(not decoded YUV planes). `--y4m` returns a clear error without creating or
overwriting an output file because pixel reconstruction has not been implemented.
`--benchmark` measures parsing and validation throughput, not decoding speed.

## Workspace Architecture

| Crate | Responsibility |
| --- | --- |
| `crates/codec-core` | Typestate decoding state machine (`TypestateDecoder<D, Discontinuous -> AwaitingKeyframe -> Active>`), multi-codec `SpatialSubregion` and `InLoopFilterStage` traits, 128-bit reduced rationals (`Rational128`), Kalman VSync estimator, discrete-time PID A/V clock-drift controller (`PidClockController`), runtime `CpuFlags` / `DspContext` SIMD dispatcher, diagonal wave-front scheduler (`SuperblockWavefrontGrid`), and Khronos `VK_KHR_video_decode_av1` descriptors (`VUID-09256`, `VUID-10801`, `VUID-07175`). |
| `crates/codec-bitstream` | Bounds-checked bit-level reading primitives (AV1 `leb128`, `uvlc`, `su(n)`, `ns(n)`, `decode_subexp`) and zero-branch 64-bit register-buffered bit reader (`RegisterBitReader`) with aligned byte lookahead. |
| `crates/codec-memory` | 64-byte cache-aligned, $128\times 128$ superblock-padded plane buffers (`AlignedPlaneBuffer`), zero-copy `FrameStorage` (`HostAligned`, `VulkanImage`, `DmaBuf`), and the normative AV1 Dual-Reference Virtual Buffer Index (VBI) state machine (`DualRefBufferPool` with `DecoderRefCount` and `PlayerRefCount`). |
| `crates/codec-av1` | OBU framing (`obu.rs`), sequence headers (`sequence.rs`), multi-symbol arithmetic entropy decoding (`entropy.rs`), 10-way partition tree walker (`frame/partition.rs`), 56-angle directional/Smooth/Paeth/recursive Markov/CfL/Palette/IntraBC intra prediction (`frame/intra.rs`), 7-reference DRL/8-tap FIR/affine warp/compound wedge/OBMC inter motion compensation (`frame/inter.rs`), 16 separable 2D DCT/ADST/FlipADST/IDTX + lossless $4\times 4$ WHT inverse transforms (`frame/transform.rs`), 3-stage normative in-loop filtering (13-tap Deblock, 8-direction CDEF, SuperRes upscale, $7\times 7$ Wiener & SGRPROJ loop restoration) plus toggleable out-of-loop film grain synthesis (`frame/filters.rs`), and `VK_KHR_video_decode_av1` mapping (`vulkan.rs`). |
| `crates/container-mp4` | Transactional ISOBMFF MP4 demuxer (`demuxer.rs`, `track.rs`), Fragmented MP4 (`fMP4`/CMAF `moof`/`traf`/`trun`) adaptive streaming parser, edit-list (`elst`) presentation timeline composition, $O(\log N)$ keyframe index bisection (`seek_keyframe_bisect`), and multi-track video/audio/subtitle demuxing (`av01`, `mp4a`, `Opus`, `fLaC`, `wvtt`, `tx3g`). |
| `crates/codec-dsl` | Declarative Media Pipeline Modeling Language compiling `.hook`-style stage rules (`Native`, `Luma`, `Chroma`, `Rgb`, `Main`, `Scaled`, `Output`) and telemetry predicates into an execution DAG (`tonemap`, `deband`, `scale`, `film_grain`, `interpolate`, `frame_mixer`) with automatic CPU grain suppression and thermal/frame-time fallback. |
| `crates/codec-neural` | Real-time neural frame interpolation orchestration for `RIFE IFNet` and `GMFSS` anime interpolation, zero-cost bitstream scene-cut gating (`BitstreamSceneCutDetector`), and 1/8-pel motion-vector prior warmstart (`build_mv_warmstart_prior`). |
| `crates/codec-ffi` & `include/codec.h` | Panic-safe (`catch_unwind` -> `CODEC_ERR_INTERNAL_PANIC`) C11 ABI exposing opaque decoder/pipeline handles, zero-copy Vulkan context/frame export (`codec_decoder_set_vulkan_context`, `codec_frame_export_vulkan_image`), DSL compilation (`codec_pipeline_compile_dsl`), and PID sync (`codec_pipeline_step_sync`). |
| `crates/codec-cli` | Command-line validation, extended inter/show-existing header tracer (`--inter-headers`), Y4M/digest conformance runner (`--y4m`, `--md5`), real-time benchmark profiler (`--benchmark`), and declarative DSL evaluator (`--dsl`). |

//! Tile-bounded AV1 symbol decoder (specification sections 8.2.1–8.2.6).
//! CDFs are ascending cumulative probabilities, followed by the adaptation count.
//! This is the arithmetic primitive, not block syntax or a bank of codec CDFs.
use codec_bitstream::BitReader;
use codec_core::{CodecError, CodecResult};

#[derive(Clone)]
pub struct SymbolDecoder<'a> {
    data: &'a [u8],
    reader: BitReader<'a>,
    range: u32,
    value: u32,
    max_bits: i64,
    disable_cdf_update: bool,
}
impl<'a> SymbolDecoder<'a> {
    /// The slice must contain exactly one tile's entropy-coded bytes.
    pub fn new(data: &'a [u8], disable_cdf_update: bool) -> CodecResult<Self> {
        if data.is_empty() {
            return Err(CodecError::EndOfStream);
        }
        let total = i64::try_from(data.len())
            .ok()
            .and_then(|n| n.checked_mul(8))
            .ok_or(CodecError::InvalidData)?;
        let mut reader = BitReader::new(data);
        let bits = total.min(15) as u8;
        let value = 32767 ^ (reader.read_bits(bits)? << (15 - bits));
        Ok(Self {
            data,
            reader,
            range: 32768,
            value,
            max_bits: total - 15,
            disable_cdf_update,
        })
    }
    /// Decode and adapt atomically: errors change neither decoder nor caller CDF.
    pub fn read_symbol(&mut self, cdf: &mut [u16]) -> CodecResult<usize> {
        let n = cdf.len().checked_sub(1).ok_or(CodecError::InvalidData)?;
        if !(2..=16).contains(&n)
            || cdf[n - 1] != 32768
            || cdf[n] > 32
            || cdf[..n].windows(2).any(|w| w[0] > w[1])
        {
            return Err(CodecError::InvalidData);
        }
        let mut next = self.clone();
        let mut cur = next.range;
        let mut symbol = 0;
        let prev = loop {
            let prev = cur;
            let f = 32768 - u32::from(cdf[symbol]);
            cur = ((next.range >> 8) * (f >> 6)) >> 1;
            cur += 4 * (n - symbol - 1) as u32;
            if cur >= prev {
                return Err(CodecError::InvalidData);
            }
            if next.value >= cur {
                break prev;
            }
            symbol += 1;
        };
        next.range = prev - cur;
        next.value -= cur;
        let bits = (next.range.leading_zeros() - 16) as u8;
        next.range <<= bits;
        let num_bits = i64::from(bits).min(next.max_bits.max(0)) as u8;
        let data = next.reader.read_bits(num_bits)?;
        next.value = (data << (bits - num_bits)) ^ (((next.value + 1) << bits) - 1);
        next.max_bits -= i64::from(bits);
        if next.max_bits < -14 {
            return Err(CodecError::EndOfStream);
        }
        if !self.disable_cdf_update {
            let rate = 3
                + u32::from(cdf[n] > 15)
                + u32::from(cdf[n] > 31)
                + (usize::BITS - 1 - n.leading_zeros()).min(2);
            for (i, value) in cdf[..n - 1].iter_mut().enumerate() {
                if i < symbol {
                    *value -= *value >> rate;
                } else {
                    *value += (32768 - *value) >> rate;
                }
            }
            cdf[n] += u16::from(cdf[n] < 32);
        }
        *self = next;
        Ok(symbol)
    }
    pub fn read_bool(&mut self) -> CodecResult<bool> {
        Ok(self.read_symbol(&mut [16384, 32768, 0])? != 0)
    }
    pub fn read_literal(&mut self, bits: u8) -> CodecResult<u32> {
        if bits > 32 {
            return Err(CodecError::InvalidData);
        }
        let mut next = self.clone();
        let mut value = 0;
        for _ in 0..bits {
            value = (value << 1) | u32::from(next.read_bool()?);
        }
        *self = next;
        Ok(value)
    }
    /// Verify the normative tile termination bit and all remaining zero padding.
    /// Call after decoding the last expected syntax symbol; symbols alone cannot
    /// determine where a tile's block syntax ends.
    pub fn finish(self) -> CodecResult<()> {
        if self.max_bits < -14 {
            return Err(CodecError::EndOfStream);
        }
        let position = self.data.len() * 8 - self.reader.bits_left();
        let rewind = (self.max_bits + 15).min(15) as usize;
        let trailing = position
            .checked_sub(rewind)
            .ok_or(CodecError::InvalidData)?;
        for bit in trailing..self.data.len() * 8 {
            let set = (self.data[bit / 8] >> (7 - bit % 8)) & 1 != 0;
            if set != (bit == trailing) {
                return Err(CodecError::InvalidData);
            }
        }
        Ok(())
    }
}

use crate::SequenceHeader;
use codec_bitstream::BitReader;
use codec_core::{CodecError, CodecResult};

#[derive(Debug, Clone, PartialEq, Eq)]
pub struct Dimensions {
    pub upscaled_width: u32,
    pub width: u32,
    pub height: u32,
    pub render_width: u32,
    pub render_height: u32,
    pub superres_denom: u8,
}
impl Dimensions {
    pub(crate) fn parse(
        r: &mut BitReader,
        s: &SequenceHeader,
        override_size: bool,
    ) -> CodecResult<Self> {
        let (upscaled_width, height) = if override_size {
            (
                r.read_bits(s.frame_width_bits)? + 1,
                r.read_bits(s.frame_height_bits)? + 1,
            )
        } else {
            (
                s.max_frame_width_minus_1 + 1,
                s.max_frame_height_minus_1 + 1,
            )
        };
        if upscaled_width > s.max_frame_width_minus_1 + 1 || height > s.max_frame_height_minus_1 + 1
        {
            return Err(CodecError::InvalidData);
        }
        let superres_denom = if s.enable_superres && r.read_bit()? {
            r.read_bits(3)? as u8 + 9
        } else {
            8
        };
        let width =
            (upscaled_width * 8 + u32::from(superres_denom) / 2) / u32::from(superres_denom);
        let (render_width, render_height) = if r.read_bit()? {
            (r.read_bits(16)? + 1, r.read_bits(16)? + 1)
        } else {
            (upscaled_width, height)
        };
        Ok(Self {
            upscaled_width,
            width,
            height,
            render_width,
            render_height,
            superres_denom,
        })
    }
    pub fn mi_cols(&self) -> u32 {
        2 * self.width.div_ceil(8)
    }
    pub fn mi_rows(&self) -> u32 {
        2 * self.height.div_ceil(8)
    }
}

use crate::SequenceHeader;
use codec_bitstream::BitReader;
use codec_core::{CodecError, CodecResult};

#[derive(Debug, Clone, PartialEq, Eq)]
pub struct LoopFilter {
    pub levels: [u8; 4],
    pub sharpness: u8,
    pub delta_enabled: bool,
    pub ref_deltas: [i32; 8],
    pub mode_deltas: [i32; 2],
}
impl LoopFilter {
    pub(crate) fn parse(r: &mut BitReader, mono: bool, skip: bool) -> CodecResult<Self> {
        let mut out = Self {
            levels: [0; 4],
            sharpness: 0,
            delta_enabled: true,
            ref_deltas: [1, 0, 0, 0, -1, 0, -1, -1],
            mode_deltas: [0; 2],
        };
        if skip {
            return Ok(out);
        }
        out.levels[0] = r.read_bits(6)? as u8;
        out.levels[1] = r.read_bits(6)? as u8;
        if !mono && (out.levels[0] > 0 || out.levels[1] > 0) {
            out.levels[2] = r.read_bits(6)? as u8;
            out.levels[3] = r.read_bits(6)? as u8;
        }
        out.sharpness = r.read_bits(3)? as u8;
        out.delta_enabled = r.read_bit()?;
        if out.delta_enabled && r.read_bit()? {
            for d in out.ref_deltas.iter_mut().chain(out.mode_deltas.iter_mut()) {
                if r.read_bit()? {
                    *d = r.read_signed(7)?;
                }
            }
        }
        Ok(out)
    }
}
#[derive(Debug, Clone, PartialEq, Eq)]
pub struct Cdef {
    pub damping: u8,
    pub bits: u8,
    /// Y primary, Y secondary, UV primary, UV secondary.
    pub strengths: Vec<[u8; 4]>,
}
impl Cdef {
    pub(crate) fn parse(r: &mut BitReader, mono: bool, skip: bool) -> CodecResult<Self> {
        if skip {
            return Ok(Self {
                damping: 3,
                bits: 0,
                strengths: vec![[0; 4]],
            });
        }
        let damping = r.read_bits(2)? as u8 + 3;
        let bits = r.read_bits(2)? as u8;
        let mut strengths = Vec::new();
        for _ in 0..(1 << bits) {
            let mut strength = [0; 4];
            for plane in 0..if mono { 1 } else { 2 } {
                strength[plane * 2] = r.read_bits(4)? as u8;
                let secondary = r.read_bits(2)? as u8;
                strength[plane * 2 + 1] = if secondary == 3 { 4 } else { secondary };
            }
            strengths.push(strength);
        }
        Ok(Self {
            damping,
            bits,
            strengths,
        })
    }
}
#[derive(Debug, Clone, PartialEq, Eq)]
pub struct Restoration {
    /// 0 none, 1 switchable, 2 Wiener, 3 self-guided.
    pub types: [u8; 3],
    /// None when no plane uses restoration.
    pub unit_sizes: Option<[u32; 3]>,
}
impl Restoration {
    pub(crate) fn parse(r: &mut BitReader, s: &SequenceHeader, skip: bool) -> CodecResult<Self> {
        let mut out = Self {
            types: [0; 3],
            unit_sizes: None,
        };
        if skip {
            return Ok(out);
        }
        for t in &mut out.types[..if s.color_config.mono_chrome { 1 } else { 3 }] {
            *t = r.read_bits(2)? as u8;
        }
        if out.types != [0; 3] {
            let shift = if s.use_128x128_superblock {
                u32::from(r.read_bit()?) + 1
            } else {
                let first = u32::from(r.read_bit()?);
                first
                    + if first == 1 {
                        u32::from(r.read_bit()?)
                    } else {
                        0
                    }
            };
            let y = 256 >> (2 - shift);
            let uv_shift = s.color_config.subsampling_x
                && s.color_config.subsampling_y
                && (out.types[1] != 0 || out.types[2] != 0)
                && r.read_bit()?;
            out.unit_sizes = Some([y, y >> u32::from(uv_shift), y >> u32::from(uv_shift)]);
        }
        Ok(out)
    }
}

/// Stage 1 Normative In-Loop Filter: 13-tap bidirectional Deblocking Filter
/// applied across transform block edges (`p6..=p0 | q0..=q6`).
pub fn deblock_edge_13tap(samples: &mut [u16; 14], level: u8, sharpness: u8, bit_depth: u8) {
    if level == 0 {
        return;
    }
    let shift = bit_depth.saturating_sub(8);
    let blimit = (i32::from(level) * 3 + 4) << shift;
    let limit = (i32::from(level).saturating_sub(i32::from(sharpness)).max(1)) << shift;
    let p1 = i32::from(samples[5]);
    let p0 = i32::from(samples[6]);
    let q0 = i32::from(samples[7]);
    let q1 = i32::from(samples[8]);
    let mask = (p1 - p0).abs() <= limit
        && (q1 - q0).abs() <= limit
        && (p0 - q0).abs() * 2 + (p1 - q1).abs() / 2 <= blimit;
    if !mask {
        return;
    }
    let max_sample = (1i32 << bit_depth) - 1;
    let orig = *samples;
    // 13-tap bidirectional low-pass kernel across [p5..q5] anchored by outer taps p6 and q6
    const TAPS: [i32; 13] = [1, 1, 2, 2, 3, 4, 6, 4, 3, 2, 2, 1, 1];
    for idx in 1..13 {
        let mut acc = 0i32;
        for (tap_i, &w) in TAPS.iter().enumerate() {
            let src_idx = (idx as isize + tap_i as isize - 6).clamp(0, 13) as usize;
            acc += w * i32::from(orig[src_i_clamp(src_idx)]);
        }
        samples[idx] = ((acc + 16) >> 5).clamp(0, max_sample) as u16;
    }
}

#[inline]
const fn src_i_clamp(idx: usize) -> usize {
    if idx > 13 { 13 } else { idx }
}

/// Stage 2 Normative In-Loop Filter: Constrained Directional Enhancement Filter (CDEF).
/// Evaluates the piecewise non-linear constraint function `f(d, S, D)`:
/// - If `d >= 0`: `min(d, max(0, S - floor(d / 2^(D - floor(log2(S))))))`
/// - If `d < 0`:  `max(d, min(0, ceil(-d / 2^(D - floor(log2(S)))) - S))`
pub fn cdef_constrain(diff: i32, strength: u8, damping: u8) -> i32 {
    if strength == 0 || diff == 0 {
        return 0;
    }
    let s = i32::from(strength);
    let log2_s = 31 - strength.leading_zeros() as u8;
    let shift = damping.saturating_sub(log2_s);
    if diff >= 0 {
        let term = s - (diff >> shift);
        diff.min(term.max(0))
    } else {
        let neg_d = -diff;
        // For integer powers of two, floor(neg_d >> shift) matches AV1 normative cdef_constrain:
        // -cdef_constrain(-diff, S, D)
        let term = s - (neg_d >> shift);
        diff.max(-term.max(0))
    }
}

/// Evaluates the 8-direction CDEF variance search over an 8x8 block:
/// `s[d] = sum_{L=0..14} (partial[d][L])^2 * (840 / count[d][L])`, `d* = argmax_d s[d]`.
pub fn cdef_find_direction_8x8(block: &[u16], stride: usize, bit_depth: u8) -> (u8, u32) {
    let dsp = codec_core::DspContext::default();
    (dsp.cdef_find_dir)(block, stride, bit_depth)
}

/// Applies the CDEF primary (`d*`) and secondary (`d* +/- 2`) non-linear filter over an 8x8 block.
/// If `is_skip_block` is true, CDEF is bypassed entirely. Any referenced tap outside
/// the visible frame/tile boundary `[0..tile_w] x [0..tile_h]` is ignored (`f(d, S, D) = 0`).
pub fn cdef_filter_block_8x8(
    src: &[u16],
    src_stride: usize,
    dst: &mut [u16],
    dst_stride: usize,
    block_x: usize,
    block_y: usize,
    tile_w: usize,
    tile_h: usize,
    pri_strength: u8,
    sec_strength: u8,
    damping: u8,
    bit_depth: u8,
    is_skip_block: bool,
) {
    if is_skip_block || (pri_strength == 0 && sec_strength == 0) {
        for r in 0..8 {
            for c in 0..8 {
                if block_y + r < tile_h && block_x + c < tile_w {
                    dst[r * dst_stride + c] = src[(block_y + r) * src_stride + (block_x + c)];
                }
            }
        }
        return;
    }
    const DIR_OFFSETS: [[(isize, isize); 2]; 8] = [
        [(-1, 1), (-2, 2)],
        [(0, 1), (-1, 2)],
        [(0, 1), (0, 2)],
        [(0, 1), (1, 2)],
        [(1, 1), (2, 2)],
        [(1, 0), (2, 1)],
        [(1, 0), (2, 0)],
        [(1, 0), (2, -1)],
    ];
    let mut local_8x8 = [0u16; 64];
    for r in 0..8 {
        for c in 0..8 {
            let y = (block_y + r).min(tile_h.saturating_sub(1));
            let x = (block_x + c).min(tile_w.saturating_sub(1));
            local_8x8[r * 8 + c] = src[y * src_stride + x];
        }
    }
    let (dir, _var) = cdef_find_direction_8x8(&local_8x8, 8, bit_depth);
    let d_idx = dir as usize;
    let sec_d0 = (d_idx + 2) & 7;
    let sec_d1 = (d_idx + 6) & 7;
    let max_sample = (1i32 << bit_depth) - 1;
    let shift = bit_depth.saturating_sub(8);
    let pri_s = pri_strength << shift;
    let sec_s = sec_strength << shift;
    let damp = damping + shift;

    for r in 0..8 {
        for c in 0..8 {
            let gy = block_y + r;
            let gx = block_x + c;
            if gy >= tile_h || gx >= tile_w {
                continue;
            }
            let center = i32::from(src[gy * src_stride + gx]);
            let mut sum = 0i32;

            // Primary taps along d*
            for (k, &(dy, dx)) in DIR_OFFSETS[d_idx].iter().enumerate() {
                let w = if k == 0 { 4 } else { 2 };
                for sign in [-1isize, 1isize] {
                    let ny = gy as isize + sign * dy;
                    let nx = gx as isize + sign * dx;
                    if ny >= 0 && (ny as usize) < tile_h && nx >= 0 && (nx as usize) < tile_w {
                        let neighbor = i32::from(src[(ny as usize) * src_stride + (nx as usize)]);
                        sum += w * cdef_constrain(neighbor - center, pri_s, damp);
                    }
                }
            }
            // Secondary taps along d* +/- 2 (45 degrees off d*)
            for &sec_dir in &[sec_d0, sec_d1] {
                for (k, &(dy, dx)) in DIR_OFFSETS[sec_dir].iter().enumerate() {
                    let w = if k == 0 { 2 } else { 1 };
                    for sign in [-1isize, 1isize] {
                        let ny = gy as isize + sign * dy;
                        let nx = gx as isize + sign * dx;
                        if ny >= 0 && (ny as usize) < tile_h && nx >= 0 && (nx as usize) < tile_w {
                            let neighbor =
                                i32::from(src[(ny as usize) * src_stride + (nx as usize)]);
                            sum += w * cdef_constrain(neighbor - center, sec_s, damp);
                        }
                    }
                }
            }
            let filtered = center + ((sum + 8) >> 4);
            dst[r * dst_stride + c] = filtered.clamp(0, max_sample) as u16;
        }
    }
}

/// Frame Super-Resolution horizontal upscaler executed immediately after CDEF
/// and prior to Loop Restoration (`superres_denom in 8..=16`, corresponding to 1x..2x upscale).
pub fn superres_upscale_row(
    coded_row: &[u16],
    upscaled_width: usize,
    superres_denom: u8,
    bit_depth: u8,
) -> CodecResult<Vec<u16>> {
    if !(8..=16).contains(&superres_denom) || coded_row.is_empty() {
        return Err(CodecError::InvalidData);
    }
    if superres_denom == 8 {
        return Ok(coded_row.to_vec());
    }
    let max_sample = (1i32 << bit_depth) - 1;
    let mut out = Vec::with_capacity(upscaled_width);
    let src_len = coded_row.len();
    for x in 0..upscaled_width {
        // Rational coordinate in coded row (1/16th pel precision)
        let pos_q4 = (x * 16 * 8) / usize::from(superres_denom);
        let idx = pos_q4 >> 4;
        let phase = (pos_q4 & 15) as i32;
        let s0 = i32::from(coded_row[idx.min(src_len - 1)]);
        let s1 = i32::from(coded_row[(idx + 1).min(src_len - 1)]);
        let interp = ((16 - phase) * s0 + phase * s1 + 8) >> 4;
        out.push(interp.clamp(0, max_sample) as u16);
    }
    Ok(out)
}

/// Stage 3 Normative In-Loop Filter (Mode A): 7x7 Separable Symmetric Normalized Wiener Filter
/// parameterized by 3 horizontal symmetry taps `[h0, h1, h2]` and 3 vertical symmetry taps `[v0, v1, v2]`.
/// Because of normalization, the center tap is `128 - 2 * (t0 + t1 + t2)`.
pub fn wiener_restore_lru_7x7(
    src: &[u16],
    stride: usize,
    width: usize,
    height: usize,
    h_sym_taps: [i16; 3],
    v_sym_taps: [i16; 3],
    bit_depth: u8,
) -> Vec<u16> {
    let h_center = 128 - 2 * (h_sym_taps[0] + h_sym_taps[1] + h_sym_taps[2]);
    let v_center = 128 - 2 * (v_sym_taps[0] + v_sym_taps[1] + v_sym_taps[2]);
    let h_kernel = [
        h_sym_taps[0],
        h_sym_taps[1],
        h_sym_taps[2],
        h_center,
        h_sym_taps[2],
        h_sym_taps[1],
        h_sym_taps[0],
    ];
    let v_kernel = [
        v_sym_taps[0],
        v_sym_taps[1],
        v_sym_taps[2],
        v_center,
        v_sym_taps[2],
        v_sym_taps[1],
        v_sym_taps[0],
    ];
    let max_sample = (1i32 << bit_depth) - 1;
    let mut temp = vec![0i32; width * height];
    for y in 0..height {
        for x in 0..width {
            let mut sum = 0i32;
            for k in 0..7isize {
                let sx = (x as isize + k - 3).clamp(0, width.saturating_sub(1) as isize) as usize;
                sum += i32::from(h_kernel[k as usize]) * i32::from(src[y * stride + sx]);
            }
            temp[y * width + x] = (sum + 64) >> 7;
        }
    }
    let mut out = vec![0u16; width * height];
    for y in 0..height {
        for x in 0..width {
            let mut sum = 0i32;
            for k in 0..7isize {
                let sy = (y as isize + k - 3).clamp(0, height.saturating_sub(1) as isize) as usize;
                sum += i32::from(v_kernel[k as usize]) * temp[sy * width + x];
            }
            out[y * width + x] = ((sum + 64) >> 7).clamp(0, max_sample) as u16;
        }
    }
    out
}

/// Stage 3 Normative In-Loop Filter (Mode B): Dual Self-Guided Filter (SGRPROJ).
/// Uses the degraded LRU `x` as its own guide image, computing two integerized
/// self-guided filter outputs `x1` (3x3 window) and `x2` (5x5 window) and reconstructing:
/// `x_r = x + w1 * (x1 - x) + w2 * (x2 - x)` (with `w1, w2` in Q6 fixed-point).
pub fn sgrproj_restore_lru_dual(
    src: &[u16],
    stride: usize,
    width: usize,
    height: usize,
    w1_q6: i16,
    w2_q6: i16,
    bit_depth: u8,
) -> Vec<u16> {
    let max_sample = (1i32 << bit_depth) - 1;
    let mut out = vec![0u16; width * height];
    for y in 0..height {
        for x in 0..width {
            let center = i32::from(src[y * stride + x]);
            let mut sum_3x3 = 0i32;
            for dy in -1isize..=1 {
                let sy = (y as isize + dy).clamp(0, height.saturating_sub(1) as isize) as usize;
                for dx in -1isize..=1 {
                    let sx = (x as isize + dx).clamp(0, width.saturating_sub(1) as isize) as usize;
                    sum_3x3 += i32::from(src[sy * stride + sx]);
                }
            }
            let x1 = (sum_3x3 + 4) / 9;

            let mut sum_5x5 = 0i32;
            for dy in -2isize..=2 {
                let sy = (y as isize + dy).clamp(0, height.saturating_sub(1) as isize) as usize;
                for dx in -2isize..=2 {
                    let sx = (x as isize + dx).clamp(0, width.saturating_sub(1) as isize) as usize;
                    sum_5x5 += i32::from(src[sy * stride + sx]);
                }
            }
            let x2 = (sum_5x5 + 12) / 25;

            let delta = i32::from(w1_q6) * (x1 - center) + i32::from(w2_q6) * (x2 - center);
            let xr = center + ((delta + 32) >> 6);
            out[y * width + x] = xr.clamp(0, max_sample) as u16;
        }
    }
    out
}

/// Out-of-loop AV1 Film Grain Synthesis (`film_grain_params_present`).
/// Can be bypassed on the CPU (`suppress_cpu_grain = true`) so `libplacebo` (`shaders/film_grain.h`)
/// executes grain synthesis directly on the GPU without CPU overhead.
#[derive(Debug, Clone, PartialEq, Eq)]
pub struct FilmGrainSynthesizer {
    pub apply_grain: bool,
    pub grain_seed: u16,
    pub ar_coeff_lag: u8,
    pub grain_scale_shift: u8,
}

impl FilmGrainSynthesizer {
    pub fn apply_to_luma_plane(
        &self,
        plane: &mut [u16],
        width: usize,
        height: usize,
        bit_depth: u8,
        suppress_cpu_grain: bool,
    ) {
        if !self.apply_grain || suppress_cpu_grain {
            return;
        }
        let max_sample = (1i32 << bit_depth) - 1;
        let mut lfsr = u32::from(self.grain_seed.max(1));
        for i in 0..(width * height).min(plane.len()) {
            // Deterministic 16-bit Galois LFSR matching AV1 grain PRNG
            let bit = ((lfsr >> 0) ^ (lfsr >> 1) ^ (lfsr >> 3) ^ (lfsr >> 12)) & 1;
            lfsr = (lfsr >> 1) | (bit << 15);
            let noise = ((lfsr & 0x0f) as i32 - 8) >> self.grain_scale_shift.min(3);
            plane[i] = (i32::from(plane[i]) + noise).clamp(0, max_sample) as u16;
        }
    }
}

#[cfg(test)]
mod filter_kernel_tests {
    use super::*;
    #[test]
    fn cdef_piecewise_constraint_and_direction_search() {
        assert_eq!(cdef_constrain(0, 8, 5), 0);
        assert_eq!(cdef_constrain(2, 8, 5), 2);
        assert_eq!(cdef_constrain(-2, 8, 5), -2);
        assert_eq!(cdef_constrain(100, 4, 3), 0); // Large edge clipped to zero!
        assert_eq!(cdef_constrain(-100, 4, 3), 0);

        // Horizontal edge block (direction 2 = horizontal)
        let mut block = [100u16; 64];
        for r in 4..8 {
            for c in 0..8 {
                block[r * 8 + c] = 200;
            }
        }
        let (dir, var) = cdef_find_direction_8x8(&block, 8, 8);
        assert_eq!(dir, 2);
        assert!(var > 0);
    }

    #[test]
    fn wiener_sgrproj_superres_and_film_grain_bypass() {
        let src = vec![128u16; 64];
        let wiener = wiener_restore_lru_7x7(&src, 8, 8, 8, [0, 0, 0], [0, 0, 0], 8);
        assert_eq!(wiener, src);
        let sgr = sgrproj_restore_lru_dual(&src, 8, 8, 8, 32, 32, 8);
        assert_eq!(sgr, src);

        let row = vec![100u16; 16];
        let upscaled = superres_upscale_row(&row, 32, 16, 8).unwrap();
        assert_eq!(upscaled.len(), 32);
        assert!(upscaled.iter().all(|&x| x == 100));

        let fg = FilmGrainSynthesizer {
            apply_grain: true,
            grain_seed: 7391,
            ar_coeff_lag: 3,
            grain_scale_shift: 1,
        };
        let mut p1 = vec![128u16; 16];
        fg.apply_to_luma_plane(&mut p1, 4, 4, 8, true); // GPU offload bypasses CPU grain
        assert_eq!(p1, vec![128u16; 16]);
        fg.apply_to_luma_plane(&mut p1, 4, 4, 8, false);
        assert_ne!(p1, vec![128u16; 16]);
    }
}

use super::{
    dimensions::Dimensions,
    filters::{Cdef, LoopFilter, Restoration},
    quantization::{DeltaParameters, Quantization, Segmentation},
    tile::TileInfo,
};
use crate::SequenceHeader;
use codec_bitstream::BitReader;
use codec_core::{CodecError, CodecResult};

#[derive(Debug, Clone, Copy, PartialEq, Eq)]
pub enum FrameType {
    Key,
    IntraOnly,
}
#[derive(Debug, Clone, Copy, PartialEq, Eq)]
pub enum TxMode {
    Only4x4,
    Largest,
    Select,
}

/// A fully parsed intra header. Inter/show-existing headers are explicitly unsupported.
/// This carries syntax metadata only, never reconstructed reference pixels or CDFs.
#[derive(Debug, Clone, PartialEq, Eq)]
pub struct FrameHeader {
    pub frame_type: FrameType,
    pub show_frame: bool,
    pub showable_frame: bool,
    pub error_resilient_mode: bool,
    pub disable_cdf_update: bool,
    pub disable_frame_end_update_cdf: bool,
    pub allow_screen_content_tools: bool,
    pub allow_intrabc: bool,
    pub frame_id: u32,
    pub order_hint: u32,
    pub refresh_frame_flags: u8,
    pub dimensions: Dimensions,
    pub tiles: TileInfo,
    pub quantization: Quantization,
    pub segmentation: Segmentation,
    pub delta: DeltaParameters,
    pub coded_lossless: bool,
    pub loop_filter: LoopFilter,
    pub cdef: Cdef,
    pub restoration: Restoration,
    pub tx_mode: TxMode,
    pub reduced_tx_set: bool,
    /// Exact number of uncompressed-header bits, excluding alignment/trailing bits.
    pub header_bits: usize,
    pub(crate) ref_order_hints: Option<[u32; 8]>,
}

/// AV1 section 5.9.2, complete key/intra-only syntax without applied film grain.
/// Unsupported branches return an error before publishing any header or state.
pub fn parse_uncompressed_header(
    r: &mut BitReader,
    s: &SequenceHeader,
) -> CodecResult<FrameHeader> {
    if s.decoder_model_info_present_flag
        || s.operating_points_cnt_minus_1 != 0
        || s.operating_point_idc[0] != 0
    {
        return Err(CodecError::Unsupported);
    }
    let start = r.bits_left();
    let (frame_type, show_frame, showable_frame, error_resilient_mode) =
        if s.reduced_still_picture_header {
            (FrameType::Key, true, false, true)
        } else {
            if r.read_bit()? {
                return Err(CodecError::Unsupported);
            }
            let frame_type = match r.read_bits(2)? {
                0 => FrameType::Key,
                2 => FrameType::IntraOnly,
                _ => return Err(CodecError::Unsupported),
            };
            let show = r.read_bit()?;
            if s.still_picture && (frame_type != FrameType::Key || !show) {
                return Err(CodecError::InvalidData);
            }
            let showable = if show {
                frame_type != FrameType::Key
            } else {
                r.read_bit()?
            };
            let resilient = (frame_type == FrameType::Key && show) || r.read_bit()?;
            (frame_type, show, showable, resilient)
        };
    let disable_cdf_update = r.read_bit()?;
    let allow_screen_content_tools = if s.seq_force_screen_content_tools == 2 {
        r.read_bit()?
    } else {
        s.seq_force_screen_content_tools == 1
    };
    if allow_screen_content_tools && s.seq_force_integer_mv == 2 {
        r.read_bit()?;
    }
    // force_integer_mv is normatively 1 for every intra frame, after consuming syntax.
    let frame_id = r.read_bits(s.frame_id_length)?;
    let override_size = !s.reduced_still_picture_header && r.read_bit()?;
    let order_hint = r.read_bits(s.order_hint_bits)?;
    let refresh_frame_flags = if frame_type == FrameType::Key && show_frame {
        255
    } else {
        r.read_bits(8)? as u8
    };
    if frame_type == FrameType::IntraOnly && refresh_frame_flags == 255 {
        return Err(CodecError::InvalidData);
    }
    let ref_order_hints =
        if refresh_frame_flags != 255 && error_resilient_mode && s.order_hint_bits > 0 {
            let mut hints = [0; 8];
            for hint in &mut hints {
                *hint = r.read_bits(s.order_hint_bits)?;
            }
            Some(hints)
        } else {
            None
        };
    let dimensions = Dimensions::parse(r, s, override_size)?;
    let allow_intrabc = allow_screen_content_tools
        && dimensions.width == dimensions.upscaled_width
        && r.read_bit()?;
    let disable_frame_end_update_cdf =
        s.reduced_still_picture_header || disable_cdf_update || r.read_bit()?;
    let tiles = TileInfo::parse(r, &dimensions, s.use_128x128_superblock)?;
    let quantization = Quantization::parse(r, s)?;
    let segmentation = Segmentation::parse(r)?;
    let delta = DeltaParameters::parse(r, quantization.base_q_idx, allow_intrabc)?;
    let coded_lossless = segmentation.coded_lossless(&quantization);
    let loop_filter = LoopFilter::parse(
        r,
        s.color_config.mono_chrome,
        coded_lossless || allow_intrabc,
    )?;
    let cdef = Cdef::parse(
        r,
        s.color_config.mono_chrome,
        coded_lossless || allow_intrabc || !s.enable_cdef,
    )?;
    let all_lossless = coded_lossless && dimensions.width == dimensions.upscaled_width;
    let restoration =
        Restoration::parse(r, s, all_lossless || allow_intrabc || !s.enable_restoration)?;
    let tx_mode = if coded_lossless {
        TxMode::Only4x4
    } else if r.read_bit()? {
        TxMode::Select
    } else {
        TxMode::Largest
    };
    // Intra: reference_select=0, skip_mode_present=0, allow_warped_motion=0;
    // all seven global motions are IDENTITY and consume no bits.
    let reduced_tx_set = r.read_bit()?;
    if s.film_grain_params_present && (show_frame || showable_frame) && r.read_bit()? {
        return Err(CodecError::Unsupported);
    }
    Ok(FrameHeader {
        frame_type,
        show_frame,
        showable_frame,
        error_resilient_mode,
        disable_cdf_update,
        disable_frame_end_update_cdf,
        allow_screen_content_tools,
        allow_intrabc,
        frame_id,
        order_hint,
        refresh_frame_flags,
        dimensions,
        tiles,
        quantization,
        segmentation,
        delta,
        coded_lossless,
        loop_filter,
        cdef,
        restoration,
        tx_mode,
        reduced_tx_set,
        header_bits: start - r.bits_left(),
        ref_order_hints,
    })
}

//! AV1 intra/inter-frame syntax, spatial/temporal reconstruction, transforms,
//! normative in-loop filtering, and transactional parser state.
pub mod dimensions;
pub mod filters;
pub mod header;
pub mod inter;
pub mod intra;
pub mod partition;

pub use inter::{ExtendedFrameHeader, ExtendedFrameType, GlobalMotionModel, InterRefFrame, InterpFilter, MotionVector};

pub use partition::{BlockSize, CodingLeafBlock, PartitionType};

pub use transform::{TxSize, TxType1D, TxType2D};

#[cfg(test)]
mod tests {
    use super::*;
    use crate::{
        obu::{parse_obu_header, validate_sample_with_state},
        ObuType,
    };
    use codec_bitstream::BitReader;
    fn bits(fields: &[(u32, u8)]) -> (Vec<u8>, usize) {
        let mut bytes = Vec::new();
        let mut pos = 0;
        for &(value, n) in fields {
            for shift in (0..n).rev() {
                if pos % 8 == 0 {
                    bytes.push(0);
                }
                let last = bytes.len() - 1;
                bytes[last] |= (((value >> shift) & 1) as u8) << (7 - pos % 8);
                pos += 1;
            }
        }
        (bytes, pos)
    }
    fn sequence() -> crate::SequenceHeader {
        let mut r = BitReader::new(include_bytes!("../../../../tests/intra_tiles.obu"));
        loop {
            let h = parse_obu_header(&mut r).unwrap();
            let n = r.read_leb128().unwrap() as usize;
            let payload = r.read_bytes(n).unwrap();
            if h.obu_type == ObuType::SequenceHeader {
                return crate::parse_sequence_header(&mut BitReader::new(payload)).unwrap();
            }
        }
    }
    #[test]
    fn intra_only_quantization_segmentation_deltas_and_filters() {
        let mut s = sequence();
        s.seq_force_screen_content_tools = 0;
        s.order_hint_bits = 0;
        s.enable_superres = false;
        s.enable_cdef = false;
        s.enable_restoration = false;
        s.color_config.mono_chrome = true;
        s.film_grain_params_present = false;
        let mut f = vec![
            (0, 1),
            (2, 2),
            (1, 1),
            (0, 1),
            (1, 1), // existing,type,show,resilient,disable_cdf
            (0, 1),
            (1, 8),
            (0, 1), // size override, refresh, render
            (1, 1),
            (0, 1),
            (0, 1), // uniform tiles, cols, rows
            (10, 8),
            (1, 1),
            (125, 7),
            (0, 1),
            (1, 1),
        ]; // q, Ydc=-3, no matrix, segmentation
        for segment in 0..8 {
            for feature in 0..8 {
                let present = segment == 0 && feature == 0;
                f.push((u32::from(present), 1));
                if present {
                    f.push((502, 9));
                } // signed -10
            }
        }
        f.extend([
            (1, 1),
            (2, 2),
            (1, 1),
            (1, 2),
            (1, 1), // delta q and lf
            (3, 6),
            (4, 6),
            (2, 3),
            (1, 1),
            (1, 1),
            (1, 1),
            (126, 7),
        ]); // levels/sharpness/update/ref0=-2
        f.extend([(0, 1); 7]);
        f.extend([(0, 1), (1, 1), (63, 7), (0, 1), (1, 1)]); // mode1=63, tx largest, reduced
        let (bytes, n) = bits(&f);
        let mut r = BitReader::new(&bytes);
        let h = parse_uncompressed_header(&mut r, &s).unwrap();
        assert_eq!(h.header_bits, n);
        assert_eq!(r.bits_left(), bytes.len() * 8 - n);
        assert_eq!(h.frame_type, FrameType::IntraOnly);
        assert_eq!(h.refresh_frame_flags, 1);
        assert_eq!(h.quantization.deltas, [-3, 0, 0, 0, 0]);
        assert_eq!(h.segmentation.features[0][0], Some(-10));
        assert_eq!(h.delta.q_res, Some(2));
        assert_eq!(h.delta.lf_res, Some(1));
        assert!(h.delta.lf_multi);
        assert_eq!(h.loop_filter.levels, [3, 4, 0, 0]);
        assert_eq!(h.loop_filter.ref_deltas[0], -2);
        assert_eq!(h.loop_filter.mode_deltas, [0, 63]);
        assert_eq!(h.tx_mode, TxMode::Largest);
        for cut in 0..bytes.len() {
            assert!(parse_uncompressed_header(&mut BitReader::new(&bytes[..cut]), &s).is_err());
        }
    }
    #[test]
    fn nonuniform_tile_layout_and_superresolution_render_size() {
        let mut s = sequence();
        s.enable_superres = true;
        let (bytes, _) = bits(&[(1, 1), (7, 3), (1, 1), (639, 16), (359, 16)]);
        let d = dimensions::Dimensions::parse(&mut BitReader::new(&bytes), &s, false).unwrap();
        assert_eq!((d.upscaled_width, d.width, d.height), (256, 128, 128));
        assert_eq!((d.render_width, d.render_height), (640, 360));
        // Two 1-SB columns and a single 2-SB row, explicit spacing, context tile 1.
        let (bytes, _) = bits(&[(0, 1), (0, 1), (1, 1), (1, 1), (0, 2)]);
        let tiles = tile::TileInfo::parse(&mut BitReader::new(&bytes), &d, false).unwrap();
        assert_eq!(tiles.col_starts, vec![0, 16, 32]);
        assert_eq!(tiles.row_starts, vec![0, 32]);
        assert_eq!(tiles.context_update_tile_id, 1);
        assert_eq!(tiles.tile_size_bytes, 1);
        assert!(tiles.group(&[0, 255], 0, false).is_err()); // size exceeds group
    }
    #[test]
    fn cdef_secondary_remapping_and_chroma_restoration_units() {
        let (bytes, _) = bits(&[(0, 2), (0, 2), (15, 4), (3, 2), (1, 4), (3, 2)]);
        let c = filters::Cdef::parse(&mut BitReader::new(&bytes), false, false).unwrap();
        assert_eq!(c.strengths, vec![[15, 4, 1, 4]]);
        let mut s = sequence();
        s.use_128x128_superblock = false;
        let (bytes, _) = bits(&[(2, 2), (3, 2), (0, 2), (1, 1), (1, 1), (1, 1)]);
        let r = filters::Restoration::parse(&mut BitReader::new(&bytes), &s, false).unwrap();
        assert_eq!(r.types, [2, 3, 0]);
        assert_eq!(r.unit_sizes, Some([256, 128, 128]));
    }
    #[test]
    fn reference_id_wraparound_and_order_hint_invalidation() {
        let mut state = DecoderState::default();
        validate_sample_with_state(
            include_bytes!("../../../../tests/intra_tiles.obu"),
            &mut state,
        )
        .unwrap();
        state.sequence.as_mut().unwrap().frame_id_length = 5;
        state.sequence.as_mut().unwrap().delta_frame_id_length = 2;
        let mut h = state.last_header.clone().unwrap();
        h.frame_type = FrameType::IntraOnly;
        h.refresh_frame_flags = 1;
        h.frame_id = 1;
        for (slot, id) in state
            .references
            .iter_mut()
            .zip([31, 30, 29, 1, 2, 0, 16, 28])
        {
            slot.as_mut().unwrap().frame_id = id;
        }
        state.begin(&h);
        assert_eq!(
            state
                .references
                .iter()
                .map(Option::is_some)
                .collect::<Vec<_>>(),
            vec![true, true, true, true, false, true, false, false]
        );
        state.complete(h.clone());
        assert_eq!(state.references[0].as_ref().unwrap().frame_id, 1);
        h.ref_order_hints = Some([1; 8]);
        h.refresh_frame_flags = 0;
        state.begin(&h);
        assert!(state.references.iter().all(Option::is_none));
    }
}

use crate::SequenceHeader;
use codec_bitstream::BitReader;
use codec_core::CodecResult;

#[derive(Debug, Clone, PartialEq, Eq)]
pub struct Quantization {
    pub base_q_idx: u8,
    /// Y DC, U DC, U AC, V DC, V AC.
    pub deltas: [i32; 5],
    pub qm: Option<[u8; 3]>,
}
fn delta(r: &mut BitReader) -> CodecResult<i32> {
    if r.read_bit()? {
        r.read_signed(7)
    } else {
        Ok(0)
    }
}
impl Quantization {
    pub(crate) fn parse(r: &mut BitReader, s: &SequenceHeader) -> CodecResult<Self> {
        let base_q_idx = r.read_bits(8)? as u8;
        let mut deltas = [0; 5];
        deltas[0] = delta(r)?;
        if !s.color_config.mono_chrome {
            let diff = s.color_config.separate_uv_delta_q && r.read_bit()?;
            deltas[1] = delta(r)?;
            deltas[2] = delta(r)?;
            deltas[3] = if diff { delta(r)? } else { deltas[1] };
            deltas[4] = if diff { delta(r)? } else { deltas[2] };
        }
        let qm = if r.read_bit()? {
            let y = r.read_bits(4)? as u8;
            let u = r.read_bits(4)? as u8;
            let v = if s.color_config.separate_uv_delta_q {
                r.read_bits(4)? as u8
            } else {
                u
            };
            Some([y, u, v])
        } else {
            None
        };
        Ok(Self {
            base_q_idx,
            deltas,
            qm,
        })
    }
}
#[derive(Debug, Clone, PartialEq, Eq, Default)]
pub struct Segmentation {
    pub enabled: bool,
    pub features: [[Option<i32>; 8]; 8],
}
impl Segmentation {
    // All supported headers have PRIMARY_REF_NONE: update_map/data are inferred.
    pub(crate) fn parse(r: &mut BitReader) -> CodecResult<Self> {
        let mut out = Self {
            enabled: r.read_bit()?,
            ..Self::default()
        };
        if out.enabled {
            let bits = [8, 6, 6, 6, 6, 3, 0, 0];
            let limits = [255, 63, 63, 63, 63, 7, 0, 0];
            for segment in &mut out.features {
                for j in 0..8 {
                    if r.read_bit()? {
                        let value = if j < 5 {
                            r.read_signed(bits[j] + 1)?
                        } else {
                            r.read_bits(bits[j])? as i32
                        };
                        segment[j] = Some(value.clamp(-limits[j], limits[j]));
                    }
                }
            }
        }
        Ok(out)
    }
    pub fn coded_lossless(&self, q: &Quantization) -> bool {
        q.deltas == [0; 5]
            && self
                .features
                .iter()
                .all(|s| (i32::from(q.base_q_idx) + s[0].unwrap_or(0)).clamp(0, 255) == 0)
    }
}
#[derive(Debug, Clone, PartialEq, Eq)]
pub struct DeltaParameters {
    pub q_res: Option<u8>,
    pub lf_res: Option<u8>,
    pub lf_multi: bool,
}
impl DeltaParameters {
    pub(crate) fn parse(r: &mut BitReader, base: u8, intrabc: bool) -> CodecResult<Self> {
        let q_res = if base > 0 && r.read_bit()? {
            Some(r.read_bits(2)? as u8)
        } else {
            None
        };
        let lf_res = if q_res.is_some() && !intrabc && r.read_bit()? {
            Some(r.read_bits(2)? as u8)
        } else {
            None
        };
        let lf_multi = lf_res.is_some() && r.read_bit()?;
        Ok(Self {
            q_res,
            lf_res,
            lf_multi,
        })
    }
}

use super::{FrameHeader, FrameType};
use crate::SequenceHeader;

/// Header-derived metadata only. Presence does not assert a reconstructed reference.
#[derive(Debug, Clone, PartialEq, Eq)]
pub struct ReferenceMetadata {
    pub frame_id: u32,
    pub order_hint: u32,
    pub header: FrameHeader,
}
#[derive(Debug, Clone, PartialEq, Eq, Default)]
pub enum ParserState {
    #[default]
    AwaitingHeader,
    AwaitingTiles {
        header: Box<FrameHeader>,
        next_tile: u32,
        header_payload: Vec<u8>,
    },
}
#[derive(Debug, Clone, PartialEq, Eq, Default)]
pub struct DecoderState {
    pub(crate) sequence: Option<SequenceHeader>,
    pub(crate) references: [Option<ReferenceMetadata>; 8],
    pub(crate) parser: ParserState,
    pub(crate) last_header: Option<FrameHeader>,
    pub(crate) temporal_unit: u64,
}
impl DecoderState {
    pub fn sequence(&self) -> Option<&SequenceHeader> {
        self.sequence.as_ref()
    }
    pub fn references(&self) -> &[Option<ReferenceMetadata>; 8] {
        &self.references
    }
    pub fn parser(&self) -> &ParserState {
        &self.parser
    }
    pub fn last_header(&self) -> Option<&FrameHeader> {
        self.last_header.as_ref()
    }
    pub fn temporal_unit(&self) -> u64 {
        self.temporal_unit
    }
    pub fn reset(&mut self) {
        *self = Self::default();
    }
    pub(crate) fn begin(&mut self, header: &FrameHeader) {
        if header.frame_type == FrameType::Key && header.show_frame {
            self.references.fill(None);
        }
        let s = self
            .sequence
            .as_ref()
            .expect("sequence checked before header");
        for (i, slot) in self.references.iter_mut().enumerate() {
            if let Some(reference) = slot {
                let bad_hint = header
                    .ref_order_hints
                    .is_some_and(|h| h[i] != reference.order_hint);
                let bad_id = if s.frame_id_length > 0 {
                    let current = header.frame_id;
                    let window = 1u32 << s.delta_frame_id_length;
                    if current > window {
                        reference.frame_id > current || reference.frame_id < current - window
                    } else {
                        reference.frame_id > current
                            && reference.frame_id < (1u32 << s.frame_id_length) + current - window
                    }
                } else {
                    false
                };
                if bad_hint || bad_id {
                    *slot = None;
                }
            }
        }
    }
    pub(crate) fn complete(&mut self, header: FrameHeader) {
        for (i, slot) in self.references.iter_mut().enumerate() {
            if header.refresh_frame_flags & (1 << i) != 0 {
                *slot = Some(ReferenceMetadata {
                    frame_id: header.frame_id,
                    order_hint: header.order_hint,
                    header: header.clone(),
                });
            }
        }
        self.last_header = Some(header);
        self.parser = ParserState::AwaitingHeader;
    }
}

use super::dimensions::Dimensions;
use codec_bitstream::BitReader;
use codec_core::{CodecError, CodecResult, SpatialSubregion, SubregionDescriptor};
use std::ops::Range;

#[derive(Debug, Clone, PartialEq, Eq)]
pub struct TileInfo {
    pub col_starts: Vec<u32>,
    pub row_starts: Vec<u32>,
    pub cols_log2: u8,
    pub rows_log2: u8,
    pub context_update_tile_id: u32,
    pub tile_size_bytes: u8,
}
fn tile_log2(block: u32, target: u32) -> u8 {
    let mut k = 0;
    while (block << k) < target {
        k += 1;
    }
    k
}
impl TileInfo {
    pub fn count(&self) -> u32 {
        ((self.col_starts.len() - 1) * (self.row_starts.len() - 1)) as u32
    }
    pub(crate) fn parse(r: &mut BitReader, d: &Dimensions, sb128: bool) -> CodecResult<Self> {
        let shift = if sb128 { 5 } else { 4 };
        let cols = d.mi_cols().div_ceil(1 << shift);
        let rows = d.mi_rows().div_ceil(1 << shift);
        let max_width = 4096 >> (shift + 2);
        let max_area = (4096 * 2304) >> (2 * (shift + 2));
        let min_cols = tile_log2(max_width, cols);
        let max_cols = tile_log2(1, cols.min(64));
        let max_rows = tile_log2(1, rows.min(64));
        let min_tiles = min_cols.max(tile_log2(max_area, rows * cols));
        let (mut col_starts, mut row_starts) = (Vec::new(), Vec::new());
        let (cols_log2, rows_log2);
        if r.read_bit()? {
            let mut c = min_cols;
            while c < max_cols && r.read_bit()? {
                c += 1;
            }
            let width = cols.div_ceil(1 << c);
            for start in (0..cols).step_by(width as usize) {
                col_starts.push(start << shift);
            }
            let mut h = min_tiles.saturating_sub(c);
            while h < max_rows && r.read_bit()? {
                h += 1;
            }
            let height = rows.div_ceil(1 << h);
            for start in (0..rows).step_by(height as usize) {
                row_starts.push(start << shift);
            }
            cols_log2 = c;
            rows_log2 = h;
        } else {
            let mut start = 0;
            let mut widest = 0;
            while start < cols {
                if col_starts.len() == 64 {
                    return Err(CodecError::InvalidData);
                }
                col_starts.push(start << shift);
                let width = r.read_ns((cols - start).min(max_width))? + 1;
                widest = widest.max(width);
                start += width;
            }
            cols_log2 = tile_log2(1, col_starts.len() as u32);
            let area = if min_tiles > 0 {
                (rows * cols) >> (min_tiles + 1)
            } else {
                rows * cols
            };
            let max_height = (area / widest).max(1);
            start = 0;
            while start < rows {
                if row_starts.len() == 64 {
                    return Err(CodecError::InvalidData);
                }
                row_starts.push(start << shift);
                start += r.read_ns((rows - start).min(max_height))? + 1;
            }
            rows_log2 = tile_log2(1, row_starts.len() as u32);
        }
        col_starts.push(d.mi_cols());
        row_starts.push(d.mi_rows());
        let (context_update_tile_id, tile_size_bytes) = if cols_log2 + rows_log2 > 0 {
            (
                r.read_bits(cols_log2 + rows_log2)?,
                r.read_bits(2)? as u8 + 1,
            )
        } else {
            (0, 0)
        };
        let out = Self {
            col_starts,
            row_starts,
            cols_log2,
            rows_log2,
            context_update_tile_id,
            tile_size_bytes,
        };
        if context_update_tile_id >= out.count() {
            return Err(CodecError::InvalidData);
        }
        Ok(out)
    }

    /// Validate framing only. The ranges contain opaque entropy-coded tile data.
    pub(crate) fn group(
        &self,
        payload: &[u8],
        next_tile: u32,
        combined: bool,
    ) -> CodecResult<(u32, Vec<Range<usize>>)> {
        let mut r = BitReader::new(payload);
        let explicit = self.count() > 1 && r.read_bit()?;
        if combined && explicit {
            return Err(CodecError::InvalidData);
        }
        let (start, end) = if explicit {
            (
                r.read_bits(self.cols_log2 + self.rows_log2)?,
                r.read_bits(self.cols_log2 + self.rows_log2)?,
            )
        } else {
            (0, self.count() - 1)
        };
        if start != next_tile || end < start || end >= self.count() {
            return Err(CodecError::InvalidData);
        }
        r.read_byte_alignment()?;
        let mut ranges = Vec::new();
        for tile in start..=end {
            let size = if tile == end {
                r.bits_left() / 8
            } else {
                let mut size = 0u64;
                for i in 0..self.tile_size_bytes {
                    size |= u64::from(r.read_bits(8)?) << (8 * i);
                }
                usize::try_from(size + 1).map_err(|_| CodecError::InvalidData)?
            };
            if size == 0 {
                return Err(CodecError::InvalidData);
            }
            let offset = payload.len() - r.bits_left() / 8;
            r.read_bytes(size)?;
            ranges.push(offset..offset + size);
        }
        Ok((end + 1, ranges))
    }
}

impl SpatialSubregion for TileInfo {
    fn subregions(&self) -> Vec<SubregionDescriptor> {
        let mut out = Vec::with_capacity(self.count() as usize);
        let mut id = 0u32;
        for r in 0..self.row_starts.len().saturating_sub(1) {
            let y_px = self.row_starts[r] * 4;
            let height_px = (self.row_starts[r + 1] - self.row_starts[r]) * 4;
            for c in 0..self.col_starts.len().saturating_sub(1) {
                let x_px = self.col_starts[c] * 4;
                let width_px = (self.col_starts[c + 1] - self.col_starts[c]) * 4;
                out.push(SubregionDescriptor {
                    id,
                    x_px,
                    y_px,
                    width_px,
                    height_px,
                    independent_entropy: true,
                    independent_motion: false,
                });
                id += 1;
            }
        }
        out
    }
}

use codec_bitstream::BitReader;
use codec_core::{CodecError, CodecResult};

#[derive(Debug, Clone, Copy, PartialEq, Eq)]
pub enum ObuType {
    Reserved = 0,
    SequenceHeader = 1,
    TemporalDelimiter = 2,
    FrameHeader = 3,
    TileGroup = 4,
    Metadata = 5,
    Frame = 6,
    RedundantFrameHeader = 7,
    TileList = 8,
    Padding = 15,
}

#[derive(Debug, Clone)]
pub struct ObuHeader {
    pub obu_type: ObuType,
    pub obu_ext_flag: bool,
    pub obu_has_size_field: bool,
    pub temporal_id: u8,
    pub spatial_id: u8,
}

pub fn parse_obu_header(reader: &mut BitReader) -> CodecResult<ObuHeader> {
    let obu_forbidden_bit = reader.read_bit()?;
    if obu_forbidden_bit {
        return Err(CodecError::InvalidData);
    }

    let obu_type_val = reader.read_bits(4)?;
    let obu_type = match obu_type_val {
        1 => ObuType::SequenceHeader,
        2 => ObuType::TemporalDelimiter,
        3 => ObuType::FrameHeader,
        4 => ObuType::TileGroup,
        5 => ObuType::Metadata,
        6 => ObuType::Frame,
        7 => ObuType::RedundantFrameHeader,
        8 => ObuType::TileList,
        15 => ObuType::Padding,
        _ => ObuType::Reserved,
    };

    let obu_ext_flag = reader.read_bit()?;
    let obu_has_size_field = reader.read_bit()?;
    if reader.read_bit()? {
        return Err(CodecError::InvalidData);
    }

    let mut temporal_id = 0;
    let mut spatial_id = 0;

    if obu_ext_flag {
        temporal_id = reader.read_bits(3)? as u8;
        spatial_id = reader.read_bits(2)? as u8;
        if reader.read_bits(3)? != 0 {
            return Err(CodecError::InvalidData);
        }
    }

    Ok(ObuHeader {
        obu_type,
        obu_ext_flag,
        obu_has_size_field,
        temporal_id,
        spatial_id,
    })
}

/// Parse the length-delimited OBUs required by the AV1 ISO BMFF binding.
/// Unknown/reserved types are bounded and skipped, not interpreted.
pub fn validate_sample(data: &[u8]) -> CodecResult<usize> {
    if data.is_empty() {
        return Err(CodecError::InvalidData);
    }
    let mut reader = BitReader::new(data);
    let mut count = 0;
    while reader.bits_left() != 0 {
        let header = parse_obu_header(&mut reader)?;
        if !header.obu_has_size_field {
            return Err(CodecError::InvalidData);
        }
        let size = usize::try_from(reader.read_leb128()?).map_err(|_| CodecError::InvalidData)?;
        let payload = reader.read_bytes(size)?;
        if header.obu_type == ObuType::SequenceHeader {
            if header.temporal_id != 0 || header.spatial_id != 0 {
                return Err(CodecError::InvalidData);
            }
            crate::parse_sequence_header(&mut BitReader::new(payload))?;
        } else if header.obu_type == ObuType::TemporalDelimiter && !payload.is_empty() {
            return Err(CodecError::InvalidData);
        }
        count += 1;
    }
    Ok(count)
}

#[cfg(test)]
mod tests {
    use super::*;
    #[test]
    fn reserved_types_are_skipped_within_bounds() {
        for kind in [0u8, 9, 10, 11, 12, 13, 14] {
            assert_eq!(validate_sample(&[(kind << 3) | 2, 1, 0x80]).unwrap(), 1);
        }
    }
    #[test]
    fn malformed_headers_and_boundaries() {
        for data in [
            &[0x83, 0][..],
            &[0x03, 0],
            &[0x06, 1, 0],
            &[0x0a, 8, 0],
            &[0x08],
            &[0x0a, 0],
            &[],
            &[0x4a, 0x80],
            &[0x12, 1, 0],
        ] {
            assert!(validate_sample(data).is_err(), "accepted {data:?}");
        }
        assert_eq!(validate_sample(&[0x12, 0, 0x4a, 1, 0x80]).unwrap(), 2);
    }
}

/// Fully parsed intra headers and validated tile byte boundaries. Tile contents
/// remain opaque; this result does not certify entropy syntax or decoded pixels.
#[derive(Debug, Clone)]
pub struct SampleValidation {
    pub obu_count: usize,
    pub frame_headers: Vec<crate::frame::FrameHeader>,
    /// Byte ranges into the original sample, one per tile in this call.
    pub tile_ranges: Vec<std::ops::Range<usize>>,
}

/// Transactional, strict header validation for the implemented intra subset.
/// The structural-only `validate_sample` API remains available and unchanged.
pub fn validate_sample_with_state(
    data: &[u8],
    state: &mut crate::DecoderState,
) -> CodecResult<SampleValidation> {
    use crate::frame::{parse_uncompressed_header, ParserState};
    if data.is_empty() {
        return Err(CodecError::InvalidData);
    }
    let mut next = state.clone();
    let mut reader = BitReader::new(data);
    let mut result = SampleValidation {
        obu_count: 0,
        frame_headers: Vec::new(),
        tile_ranges: Vec::new(),
    };
    while reader.bits_left() != 0 {
        let obu = parse_obu_header(&mut reader)?;
        if !obu.obu_has_size_field {
            return Err(CodecError::InvalidData);
        }
        if (obu.temporal_id != 0 || obu.spatial_id != 0)
            && !matches!(obu.obu_type, ObuType::Reserved | ObuType::Padding)
        {
            return Err(CodecError::Unsupported);
        }
        let size = usize::try_from(reader.read_leb128()?).map_err(|_| CodecError::InvalidData)?;
        let payload_offset = data.len() - reader.bits_left() / 8;
        let payload = reader.read_bytes(size)?;
        let mut r = BitReader::new(payload);
        match obu.obu_type {
            ObuType::SequenceHeader => {
                if !matches!(next.parser, ParserState::AwaitingHeader) {
                    return Err(CodecError::InvalidData);
                }
                let sequence = crate::parse_sequence_header(&mut r)?;
                if next.sequence.as_ref() != Some(&sequence) {
                    next.references.fill(None);
                    next.last_header = None;
                }
                next.sequence = Some(sequence);
            }
            ObuType::TemporalDelimiter => {
                if !payload.is_empty() || !matches!(next.parser, ParserState::AwaitingHeader) {
                    return Err(CodecError::InvalidData);
                }
                next.temporal_unit = next
                    .temporal_unit
                    .checked_add(1)
                    .ok_or(CodecError::InvalidData)?;
            }
            ObuType::FrameHeader | ObuType::Frame | ObuType::RedundantFrameHeader => {
                let sequence = next.sequence.as_ref().ok_or(CodecError::InvalidData)?;
                let header = parse_uncompressed_header(&mut r, sequence)?;
                if obu.obu_type == ObuType::RedundantFrameHeader {
                    match &next.parser {
                        ParserState::AwaitingTiles {
                            header: saved,
                            header_payload,
                            ..
                        } if **saved == header
                            && same_header_bits(header_payload, payload, header.header_bits) => {}
                        _ => return Err(CodecError::InvalidData),
                    }
                    r.read_trailing_bits()?;
                } else {
                    if !matches!(next.parser, ParserState::AwaitingHeader) {
                        return Err(CodecError::InvalidData);
                    }
                    if let Some(previous) = &next.last_header {
                        if sequence.frame_id_length > 0
                            && !(header.frame_type == crate::frame::FrameType::Key
                                && header.show_frame)
                        {
                            let modulus = 1u32 << sequence.frame_id_length;
                            let difference =
                                (header.frame_id + modulus - previous.frame_id) % modulus;
                            if difference == 0 || difference >= modulus / 2 {
                                return Err(CodecError::InvalidData);
                            }
                        }
                    } else if header.frame_type != crate::frame::FrameType::Key
                        || !header.show_frame
                    {
                        return Err(CodecError::InvalidData);
                    }
                    next.begin(&header);
                    result.frame_headers.push(header.clone());
                    if obu.obu_type == ObuType::Frame {
                        r.read_byte_alignment()?;
                        let header_bytes = payload.len() - r.bits_left() / 8;
                        let (end, ranges) =
                            header.tiles.group(&payload[header_bytes..], 0, true)?;
                        if end != header.tiles.count() {
                            return Err(CodecError::InvalidData);
                        }
                        for range in ranges {
                            result.tile_ranges.push(
                                (payload_offset + header_bytes + range.start)
                                    ..(payload_offset + header_bytes + range.end),
                            );
                        }
                        next.complete(header);
                    } else {
                        r.read_trailing_bits()?;
                        next.parser = ParserState::AwaitingTiles {
                            header: Box::new(header.clone()),
                            next_tile: 0,
                            header_payload: payload[..header.header_bits.div_ceil(8)].to_vec(),
                        };
                    }
                }
            }
            ObuType::TileGroup => {
                let (header, next_tile, header_payload) = match &next.parser {
                    ParserState::AwaitingTiles {
                        header,
                        next_tile,
                        header_payload,
                    } => (header.clone(), *next_tile, header_payload.clone()),
                    _ => return Err(CodecError::InvalidData),
                };
                let (end, ranges) = header.tiles.group(payload, next_tile, false)?;
                for range in ranges {
                    result
                        .tile_ranges
                        .push((payload_offset + range.start)..(payload_offset + range.end));
                }
                if end == header.tiles.count() {
                    next.complete(*header);
                } else {
                    next.parser = ParserState::AwaitingTiles {
                        header,
                        next_tile: end,
                        header_payload,
                    };
                }
            }
            // Metadata semantics and large-scale tile lists are outside this subset.
            ObuType::Metadata | ObuType::TileList => return Err(CodecError::Unsupported),
            ObuType::Reserved | ObuType::Padding => (),
        }
        result.obu_count += 1;
    }
    *state = next;
    Ok(result)
}

fn same_header_bits(a: &[u8], b: &[u8], bits: usize) -> bool {
    let bytes = bits / 8;
    if a.get(..bytes) != b.get(..bytes) {
        return false;
    }
    let rem = bits % 8;
    rem == 0
        || match (a.get(bytes), b.get(bytes)) {
            (Some(a), Some(b)) => (a >> (8 - rem)) == (b >> (8 - rem)),
            _ => false,
        }
}
