use std::cmp::Ordering;

/// Exact 128-bit reduced rational Q = i128 / u64 to prevent cumulative IEEE-754
/// rounding drift over multi-hour streams when converting container mdhd timescales
/// and AV1 timing metadata (num_units_in_display_tick / time_scale).
#[derive(Debug, Clone, Copy, PartialEq, Eq)]
pub struct Rational128 {
    pub num: i128,
    pub den: u64,
}

impl Rational128 {
    pub fn new(num: i128, den: u64) -> crate::CodecResult<Self> {
        if den == 0 {
            return Err(crate::CodecError::InvalidData);
        }
        let g = gcd_u128(num.unsigned_abs(), u128::from(den));
        let reduced_num = num / (g as i128);
        let reduced_den =
            u64::try_from(u128::from(den) / g).map_err(|_| crate::CodecError::InvalidData)?;
        Ok(Self {
            num: reduced_num,
            den: reduced_den,
        })
    }

    pub fn to_seconds_f64(self) -> f64 {
        (self.num as f64) / (self.den as f64)
    }

    pub fn checked_sub(self, other: Self) -> crate::CodecResult<Self> {
        let lhs = self
            .num
            .checked_mul(i128::from(other.den))
            .ok_or(crate::CodecError::InvalidData)?;
        let rhs = other
            .num
            .checked_mul(i128::from(self.den))
            .ok_or(crate::CodecError::InvalidData)?;
        let num = lhs.checked_sub(rhs).ok_or(crate::CodecError::InvalidData)?;
        let den = self
            .den
            .checked_mul(other.den)
            .ok_or(crate::CodecError::InvalidData)?;
        Self::new(num, den)
    }
}

const fn gcd_u128(mut a: u128, mut b: u128) -> u128 {
    while b != 0 {
        let r = a % b;
        a = b;
        b = r;
    }
    if a == 0 {
        1
    } else {
        a
    }
}

#[derive(Debug, Clone, Copy, Eq)]
pub struct Timestamp {
    pub pts: i64,
    pub time_base_num: u32,
    pub time_base_den: u32,
}

impl Timestamp {
    pub fn new(pts: i64, num: u32, den: u32) -> crate::CodecResult<Self> {
        if num == 0 || den == 0 {
            return Err(crate::CodecError::InvalidData);
        }
        Ok(Self {
            pts,
            time_base_num: num,
            time_base_den: den,
        })
    }

    /// Convert to an exact reduced 128-bit rational timestamp in seconds.
    pub fn to_rational128(&self) -> crate::CodecResult<Rational128> {
        if self.time_base_num == 0 || self.time_base_den == 0 {
            return Err(crate::CodecError::InvalidData);
        }
        let num = i128::from(self.pts) * i128::from(self.time_base_num);
        Rational128::new(num, u64::from(self.time_base_den))
    }

    /// Rescale with truncation toward zero; invalid bases and overflow are errors.
    pub fn rescale(&self, new_num: u32, new_den: u32) -> crate::CodecResult<i64> {
        if self.time_base_num == 0 || self.time_base_den == 0 || new_num == 0 || new_den == 0 {
            return Err(crate::CodecError::InvalidData);
        }
        let numerator = i128::from(self.pts)
            .checked_mul(i128::from(self.time_base_num))
            .and_then(|v| v.checked_mul(i128::from(new_den)))
            .ok_or(crate::CodecError::InvalidData)?;
        let denominator = i128::from(self.time_base_den)
            .checked_mul(i128::from(new_num))
            .ok_or(crate::CodecError::InvalidData)?;
        i64::try_from(numerator / denominator).map_err(|_| crate::CodecError::InvalidData)
    }

    fn get_exact_compare_values(&self, other: &Self) -> (i128, i128) {
        let val1 =
            (self.pts as i128) * (self.time_base_num as i128) * (other.time_base_den as i128);
        let val2 =
            (other.pts as i128) * (other.time_base_num as i128) * (self.time_base_den as i128);
        (val1, val2)
    }
}

impl PartialEq for Timestamp {
    fn eq(&self, other: &Self) -> bool {
        let (v1, v2) = self.get_exact_compare_values(other);
        v1 == v2
    }
}

impl PartialOrd for Timestamp {
    fn partial_cmp(&self, other: &Self) -> Option<Ordering> {
        Some(self.cmp(other))
    }
}

impl Ord for Timestamp {
    fn cmp(&self, other: &Self) -> Ordering {
        let (v1, v2) = self.get_exact_compare_values(other);
        v1.cmp(&v2)
    }
}

/// 1D Kalman filter estimating true display VSync refresh period T_vsync
/// and computing integer VSync cadence ticks and oversample blend weight alpha_mix.
#[derive(Debug, Clone)]
pub struct KalmanVsyncEstimator {
    pub period_sec: f64,
    pub variance: f64,
    pub process_noise: f64,
    pub measurement_noise: f64,
    pub last_vsync_sec: Option<f64>,
}

#[derive(Debug, Clone, Copy, PartialEq)]
pub struct VsyncCadenceStep {
    pub expected_vsync_sec: f64,
    pub vsync_ticks: u32,
    pub alpha_mix: f64,
}

impl KalmanVsyncEstimator {
    pub fn new(nominal_hz: f64) -> Self {
        let nominal_period = if nominal_hz.is_finite() && nominal_hz > 0.0 {
            1.0 / nominal_hz
        } else {
            0.0
        };
        let period_sec = if nominal_period.is_finite() && nominal_period > 0.0 {
            nominal_period
        } else {
            1.0 / 60.0
        };
        Self {
            period_sec,
            variance: 1e-6,
            process_noise: 1e-9,
            measurement_noise: 1e-7,
            last_vsync_sec: None,
        }
    }

    pub fn observe_vsync(&mut self, vsync_timestamp_sec: f64) {
        // An invalid display timestamp must not poison subsequent measurements.
        if !vsync_timestamp_sec.is_finite() {
            return;
        }
        if let Some(prev) = self.last_vsync_sec {
            let measured_dt = vsync_timestamp_sec - prev;
            if measured_dt > 0.001 && measured_dt < 0.1 {
                let prior_var = self.variance + self.process_noise;
                let kalman_gain = prior_var / (prior_var + self.measurement_noise);
                self.period_sec += kalman_gain * (measured_dt - self.period_sec);
                self.variance = (1.0 - kalman_gain) * prior_var;
            }
        }
        self.last_vsync_sec = Some(vsync_timestamp_sec);
    }

    pub fn map_frame_pts(&self, frame_pts_sec: f64, base_vsync_sec: f64) -> VsyncCadenceStep {
        let base = if base_vsync_sec.is_finite() {
            base_vsync_sec
        } else {
            0.0
        };
        let fallback = VsyncCadenceStep {
            expected_vsync_sec: base,
            vsync_ticks: 0,
            alpha_mix: 0.0,
        };
        if !frame_pts_sec.is_finite() {
            return fallback;
        }
        let period = if self.period_sec.is_finite() && self.period_sec > 0.0 {
            self.period_sec
        } else {
            1.0 / 60.0
        };
        let exact_ticks = (frame_pts_sec - base).max(0.0) / period;
        if !exact_ticks.is_finite() {
            return fallback;
        }
        // Avoid an inconsistent fractional blend after integer tick saturation.
        let saturated = exact_ticks >= f64::from(u32::MAX);
        let vsync_ticks = exact_ticks.floor() as u32;
        let alpha_mix = if saturated {
            0.0
        } else {
            exact_ticks - f64::from(vsync_ticks)
        };
        let expected_vsync_sec = base + f64::from(vsync_ticks) * period;
        if !expected_vsync_sec.is_finite() {
            return fallback;
        }
        VsyncCadenceStep {
            expected_vsync_sec,
            vsync_ticks,
            alpha_mix,
        }
    }
}

/// Closed-loop discrete-time Proportional-Integral-Derivative (PID) controller
/// locking audio resampling ratio r[k] to VSync-aligned video presentation cadence.
#[derive(Debug, Clone)]
pub struct PidClockController {
    pub kp: f64,
    pub ki: f64,
    pub kd: f64,
    pub delta_max: f64,
    pub integral: f64,
    pub prev_error: Option<f64>,
    /// Content-aware audio delay register (e.g., +1 frame offset for RIFE lookahead).
    pub audio_delay_offset_sec: f64,
}

#[derive(Debug, Clone, Copy, PartialEq)]
pub struct SyncStepOutput {
    pub phase_error_sec: f64,
    pub control_signal: f64,
    pub audio_resample_ratio: f64,
}

impl Default for PidClockController {
    fn default() -> Self {
        Self::new_critically_damped()
    }
}

impl PidClockController {
    /// Critically damped defaults from architectural specification:
    /// Kp = 0.015, Ki = 0.0005 s^-1, Kd = 0.002 s, delta_max = 0.005 (+/- 0.5%).
    pub fn new_critically_damped() -> Self {
        Self {
            kp: 0.015,
            ki: 0.0005,
            kd: 0.002,
            delta_max: 0.005,
            integral: 0.0,
            prev_error: None,
            audio_delay_offset_sec: 0.0,
        }
    }

    pub fn set_neural_lookahead_frames(&mut self, lookahead_frames: u32, frame_rate_hz: f64) {
        if frame_rate_hz.is_finite() && frame_rate_hz > 0.0 {
            let delay = f64::from(lookahead_frames) / frame_rate_hz;
            if delay.is_finite() {
                self.audio_delay_offset_sec = delay;
            }
        }
    }

    pub fn reset(&mut self) {
        self.integral = 0.0;
        self.prev_error = None;
    }

    fn neutral_step(&mut self) -> SyncStepOutput {
        self.reset();
        SyncStepOutput {
            phase_error_sec: 0.0,
            control_signal: 0.0,
            audio_resample_ratio: 1.0,
        }
    }

    /// Evaluates:
    /// e[k] = (PTS_v[k] - t_vsync_expected[k]) - (PTS_a[k] + delay_offset - t_dac_buffered[k])
    /// u[k] = Kp * e[k] + Ki * sum(e[j] * dt_j) + Kd * (e[k] - e[k-1]) / dt_k
    /// r[k] = clamp(1.0 + u[k], 1.0 - delta_max, 1.0 + delta_max)
    pub fn step(
        &mut self,
        pts_video_sec: f64,
        vsync_expected_sec: f64,
        pts_audio_sec: f64,
        dac_buffered_sec: f64,
        dt_sec: f64,
    ) -> SyncStepOutput {
        // Invalid clock data must not corrupt the controller's integral/derivative state.
        if ![
            pts_video_sec,
            vsync_expected_sec,
            pts_audio_sec,
            dac_buffered_sec,
            self.audio_delay_offset_sec,
            self.kp,
            self.ki,
            self.kd,
            self.delta_max,
            self.integral,
        ]
        .iter()
        .all(|value| value.is_finite())
            || self.delta_max < 0.0
        {
            return self.neutral_step();
        }
        let dt = if dt_sec.is_finite() {
            dt_sec.max(1e-4)
        } else {
            1.0 / 60.0
        };
        let video_phase = pts_video_sec - vsync_expected_sec;
        let audio_phase = (pts_audio_sec + self.audio_delay_offset_sec) - dac_buffered_sec;
        let error = video_phase - audio_phase;
        if !error.is_finite() {
            return self.neutral_step();
        }

        // Anti-windup clamped integral accumulation
        let max_integral = if self.ki > 0.0 {
            self.delta_max / self.ki
        } else {
            1.0
        };
        self.integral = (self.integral + error * dt).clamp(-max_integral, max_integral);

        let derivative = match self.prev_error {
            Some(prev) => (error - prev) / dt,
            None => 0.0,
        };
        self.prev_error = Some(error);

        let u = self.kp * error + self.ki * self.integral + self.kd * derivative;
        if !u.is_finite() {
            return self.neutral_step();
        }
        let r = (1.0 + u).clamp(1.0 - self.delta_max, 1.0 + self.delta_max);

        SyncStepOutput {
            phase_error_sec: error,
            control_signal: u,
            audio_resample_ratio: r,
        }
    }
}

#[cfg(test)]
mod tests {
    use super::*;
    #[test]
    fn wide_rescale_and_invalid_bases() {
        let t = Timestamp::new(i64::MAX, u32::MAX, u32::MAX).unwrap();
        assert_eq!(t.rescale(u32::MAX, u32::MAX).unwrap(), i64::MAX);
        assert!(Timestamp::new(1, 0, 1).is_err());
        assert!(Timestamp::new(1, 1, 0).is_err());
        assert!(t.rescale(0, 1).is_err());
        assert!(t.rescale(1, 0).is_err());
        assert!(t.rescale(1, 2).is_err());
        assert_eq!(Timestamp::new(-3, 1, 2).unwrap().rescale(1, 1).unwrap(), -1);
        assert!(Timestamp {
            pts: 1,
            time_base_num: 0,
            time_base_den: 0
        }
        .rescale(1, 1)
        .is_err());
    }
    #[test]
    fn exact_ordering() {
        assert_eq!(
            Timestamp::new(1, 1, 2).unwrap(),
            Timestamp::new(2, 1, 4).unwrap()
        );
        assert!(Timestamp::new(-1, 1, 2).unwrap() < Timestamp::new(0, 1, 1).unwrap());
    }
    #[test]
    fn rational128_and_pid_sync_controller() {
        let r1 = Rational128::new(24000, 1001).unwrap();
        let r2 = Rational128::new(48000, 2002).unwrap();
        assert_eq!(r1, r2);
        let diff = r1.checked_sub(r2).unwrap();
        assert_eq!(diff.num, 0);

        let mut pid = PidClockController::new_critically_damped();
        pid.set_neural_lookahead_frames(1, 24.0);
        assert!((pid.audio_delay_offset_sec - (1.0 / 24.0)).abs() < 1e-9);
        let out = pid.step(1.05, 1.00, 1.00, 1.00, 1.0 / 60.0);
        assert!(out.audio_resample_ratio >= 0.995 && out.audio_resample_ratio <= 1.005);

        let mut kalman = KalmanVsyncEstimator::new(60.0);
        kalman.observe_vsync(0.0);
        kalman.observe_vsync(1.0 / 60.0);
        let step = kalman.map_frame_pts(1.0 / 24.0, 0.0);
        assert_eq!(step.vsync_ticks, 2);
        assert!((step.alpha_mix - 0.5).abs() < 1e-2);
    }

    #[test]
    fn invalid_display_clock_samples_do_not_poison_estimator() {
        for bad_rate in [0.0, -60.0, f64::NAN, f64::INFINITY, f64::MAX] {
            let estimator = KalmanVsyncEstimator::new(bad_rate);
            assert!(estimator.period_sec.is_finite() && estimator.period_sec > 0.0);
            assert_eq!(estimator.map_frame_pts(0.0, 0.0).vsync_ticks, 0);
        }
        let mut estimator = KalmanVsyncEstimator::new(60.0);
        estimator.observe_vsync(0.0);
        estimator.observe_vsync(f64::NAN);
        estimator.observe_vsync(f64::INFINITY);
        assert_eq!(estimator.last_vsync_sec, Some(0.0));
        estimator.observe_vsync(1.0 / 60.0);
        assert!(estimator.period_sec.is_finite());
        let invalid = estimator.map_frame_pts(f64::NAN, 123.0);
        assert_eq!(invalid.vsync_ticks, 0);
        assert_eq!(invalid.alpha_mix, 0.0);
        assert_eq!(invalid.expected_vsync_sec, 123.0);
        let huge = estimator.map_frame_pts(1.0e12, 0.0);
        assert_eq!(huge.vsync_ticks, u32::MAX);
        assert_eq!(huge.alpha_mix, 0.0);
    }

    #[test]
    fn invalid_sync_inputs_recover_without_persistent_nan_state() {
        let mut pid = PidClockController::new_critically_damped();
        let valid = pid.step(1.05, 1.0, 1.0, 1.0, 1.0 / 60.0);
        assert!(valid.audio_resample_ratio.is_finite());
        pid.set_neural_lookahead_frames(1, f64::NAN);
        pid.set_neural_lookahead_frames(1, f64::INFINITY);
        assert_eq!(pid.audio_delay_offset_sec, 0.0);
        let invalid = pid.step(f64::NAN, 1.0, 1.0, 1.0, f64::NAN);
        assert_eq!(invalid.audio_resample_ratio, 1.0);
        assert_eq!(invalid.control_signal, 0.0);
        assert_eq!(pid.integral, 0.0);
        assert_eq!(pid.prev_error, None);
        let recovered = pid.step(1.05, 1.0, 1.0, 1.0, f64::NAN);
        assert!(recovered.audio_resample_ratio.is_finite());
        assert!(pid.integral.is_finite());
    }
}

use codec_core::{CodecId, DecoderConfig, PidClockController};
use codec_dsl::CompiledPipelineDag;
use std::ffi::CStr;
use std::os::raw::c_char;
use std::panic::catch_unwind;

/// Opaque struct type in C
#[repr(C)]
pub struct DecoderHandle {
    _private: [u8; 0],
}

#[repr(C)]
pub struct CodecFrame {
    pub image_view: u64,
    pub timeline_semaphore: u64,
    pub timeline_value: u64,
    pub dpb_slot: u8,
}

#[repr(C)]
pub struct CodecPipeline {
    _private: [u8; 0],
}

#[repr(C)]
#[derive(Debug, Clone, Copy)]
pub struct CodecVulkanConfig {
    pub device: u64,
    pub decode_queue: u64,
    pub queue_family_index: u32,
}

#[repr(C)]
#[derive(Debug, Clone, Copy)]
pub struct CodecVulkanFrameDescriptor {
    pub image_view: u64,
    pub timeline_semaphore: u64,
    pub timeline_value: u64,
    pub dpb_slot: u8,
}

#[repr(C)]
#[derive(Debug, Clone, Copy)]
pub struct CodecSyncAdjustment {
    pub phase_error_sec: f64,
    pub control_signal: f64,
    pub audio_resample_ratio: f64,
}

#[repr(C)]
pub enum FfiCodecId {
    Av1 = 0,
    H264 = 1,
}

struct DecoderState {
    #[allow(dead_code)]
    id: CodecId,
    #[allow(dead_code)]
    config: Option<DecoderConfig>,
    vulkan_config: Option<CodecVulkanConfig>,
}

struct PipelineState {
    #[allow(dead_code)]
    dag: CompiledPipelineDag,
    pid: PidClockController,
}

#[repr(C)]
pub enum FfiResult {
    Ok = 0,
    InvalidData = -1,
    Unsupported = -2,
    OutOfMemory = -3,
    InternalError = -4,
    InternalPanic = -5,
}

#[no_mangle]
pub extern "C" fn codec_get_version() -> *const c_char {
    // Return a stable string version for ABI
    c"0.1.0".as_ptr() as *const c_char
}

#[no_mangle]
/// # Safety
/// out_handle must be non-NULL and writable; failures clear *out_handle.
pub unsafe extern "C" fn codec_decoder_create(
    codec: u32,
    out_handle: *mut *mut DecoderHandle,
) -> FfiResult {
    if out_handle.is_null() {
        return FfiResult::InvalidData;
    }

    *out_handle = std::ptr::null_mut();
    let res = catch_unwind(|| {
        let id = match codec {
            0 => CodecId::Av1,
            1 => CodecId::H264,
            _ => return FfiResult::InvalidData,
        };

        if id != CodecId::Av1 {
            return FfiResult::Unsupported;
        }

        let state = Box::new(DecoderState {
            id,
            config: None,
            vulkan_config: None,
        });

        *out_handle = Box::into_raw(state) as *mut DecoderHandle;
        FfiResult::Ok
    });

    match res {
        Ok(r) => r,
        Err(_) => FfiResult::InternalPanic,
    }
}

#[no_mangle]
/// # Safety
/// Pass NULL or one live handle returned by create, exactly once.
pub unsafe extern "C" fn codec_decoder_destroy(handle: *mut DecoderHandle) {
    let _ = catch_unwind(|| {
        if !handle.is_null() {
            let _state = Box::from_raw(handle as *mut DecoderState);
        }
    });
}

#[no_mangle]
/// # Safety
/// handle and config must be valid non-NULL pointers.
pub unsafe extern "C" fn codec_decoder_set_vulkan_context(
    handle: *mut DecoderHandle,
    config: *const CodecVulkanConfig,
) -> FfiResult {
    let res = catch_unwind(|| {
        if handle.is_null() || config.is_null() {
            return FfiResult::InvalidData;
        }
        let cfg = *config;
        if cfg.device == 0 || cfg.decode_queue == 0 {
            return FfiResult::InvalidData;
        }
        let state = &mut *(handle as *mut DecoderState);
        state.vulkan_config = Some(cfg);
        FfiResult::Ok
    });
    match res {
        Ok(r) => r,
        Err(_) => FfiResult::InternalPanic,
    }
}

#[no_mangle]
/// # Safety
/// frame and out_desc must be valid non-NULL pointers.
pub unsafe extern "C" fn codec_frame_export_vulkan_image(
    frame: *const CodecFrame,
    out_desc: *mut CodecVulkanFrameDescriptor,
) -> FfiResult {
    let res = catch_unwind(|| {
        if frame.is_null() || out_desc.is_null() {
            return FfiResult::InvalidData;
        }
        let f = &*frame;
        if f.image_view == 0 || f.dpb_slot >= 8 {
            return FfiResult::InvalidData;
        }
        *out_desc = CodecVulkanFrameDescriptor {
            image_view: f.image_view,
            timeline_semaphore: f.timeline_semaphore,
            timeline_value: f.timeline_value,
            dpb_slot: f.dpb_slot,
        };
        FfiResult::Ok
    });
    match res {
        Ok(r) => r,
        Err(_) => FfiResult::InternalPanic,
    }
}

#[no_mangle]
/// # Safety
/// dsl_source must be a valid NUL-terminated UTF-8 string and out_pipeline must be non-NULL.
pub unsafe extern "C" fn codec_pipeline_compile_dsl(
    dsl_source: *const c_char,
    out_pipeline: *mut *mut CodecPipeline,
) -> FfiResult {
    if out_pipeline.is_null() {
        return FfiResult::InvalidData;
    }
    *out_pipeline = std::ptr::null_mut();
    let res = catch_unwind(|| {
        if dsl_source.is_null() {
            return FfiResult::InvalidData;
        }
        let Ok(cstr) = CStr::from_ptr(dsl_source).to_str() else {
            return FfiResult::InvalidData;
        };
        let Ok(dag) = CompiledPipelineDag::compile_str(cstr) else {
            return FfiResult::InvalidData;
        };
        let mut pid = PidClockController::new_critically_damped();
        if dag.rules.iter().any(|r| {
            matches!(
                r.operator,
                codec_dsl::DslOperator::Interpolate { .. }
            )
        }) {
            pid.set_neural_lookahead_frames(1, 24.0);
        }
        let state = Box::new(PipelineState { dag, pid });
        *out_pipeline = Box::into_raw(state) as *mut CodecPipeline;
        FfiResult::Ok
    });
    match res {
        Ok(r) => r,
        Err(_) => FfiResult::InternalPanic,
    }
}

#[no_mangle]
/// # Safety
/// pipeline and out_adj must be valid non-NULL pointers.
pub unsafe extern "C" fn codec_pipeline_step_sync(
    pipeline: *mut CodecPipeline,
    audio_pts: f64,
    vsync_pts: f64,
    out_adj: *mut CodecSyncAdjustment,
) -> FfiResult {
    let res = catch_unwind(|| {
        if pipeline.is_null() || out_adj.is_null() {
            return FfiResult::InvalidData;
        }
        let state = &mut *(pipeline as *mut PipelineState);
        let step = state.pid.step(vsync_pts, vsync_pts, audio_pts, audio_pts, 1.0 / 60.0);
        *out_adj = CodecSyncAdjustment {
            phase_error_sec: step.phase_error_sec,
            control_signal: step.control_signal,
            audio_resample_ratio: step.audio_resample_ratio,
        };
        FfiResult::Ok
    });
    match res {
        Ok(r) => r,
        Err(_) => FfiResult::InternalPanic,
    }
}

#[no_mangle]
/// # Safety
/// Pass NULL or one live pipeline handle returned by codec_pipeline_compile_dsl, exactly once.
pub unsafe extern "C" fn codec_pipeline_destroy(pipeline: *mut CodecPipeline) {
    let _ = catch_unwind(|| {
        if !pipeline.is_null() {
            let _state = Box::from_raw(pipeline as *mut PipelineState);
        }
    });
}

#include "codec.h"
#include <assert.h>
#include <stddef.h>
#include <string.h>

int main(void) {
    DecoderHandle *handle = NULL;
    CodecPipeline *pipeline = NULL;
    CodecVulkanConfig vk_cfg = { 0x1000u, 0x2000u, 0u };
    CodecSyncAdjustment sync_adj = { 0.0, 0.0, 0.0 };

    assert(strcmp(codec_get_version(), "0.1.0") == 0);
    assert(codec_decoder_create(Av1, &handle) == Ok);
    assert(handle != NULL);
    assert(codec_decoder_set_vulkan_context(handle, &vk_cfg) == Ok);
    assert(codec_decoder_set_vulkan_context(handle, NULL) == InvalidData);
    codec_decoder_destroy(handle);
    handle = NULL;

    assert(codec_decoder_create(H264, &handle) == Unsupported);
    assert(handle == NULL);
    assert(codec_decoder_create(UINT32_MAX, &handle) == InvalidData);
    assert(handle == NULL);
    assert(codec_decoder_create(Av1, NULL) == InvalidData);
    codec_decoder_destroy(NULL);

    assert(codec_pipeline_compile_dsl("film_grain(gpu: true); tonemap(curve: mobius); interpolate(model: rife_v4_tensorrt)", &pipeline) == Ok);
    assert(pipeline != NULL);
    assert(codec_pipeline_step_sync(pipeline, 1.0, 1.0, &sync_adj) == Ok);
    assert(sync_adj.audio_resample_ratio >= 0.995 && sync_adj.audio_resample_ratio <= 1.005);
    codec_pipeline_destroy(pipeline);
    codec_pipeline_destroy(NULL);

    assert(codec_pipeline_compile_dsl("", &pipeline) == InvalidData);
    assert(pipeline == NULL);
    assert(CODEC_ERR_INTERNAL_PANIC == InternalPanic);
    return 0;
}

use std::{env, fs, path::PathBuf, process::Command};

#[test]
fn test_c_abi() {
    // cargo test builds the cdylib in the test executable's dependency directory.
    let deps = env::current_exe().unwrap().parent().unwrap().to_path_buf();
    let root = PathBuf::from(env!("CARGO_MANIFEST_DIR")).join("../..");
    let out = deps.join("c-abi-check");
    fs::create_dir_all(&out).unwrap();
    let exe = out.join(if cfg!(windows) { "abi.exe" } else { "abi" });
    let compiler = env::var("CC").unwrap_or_else(|_| {
        if cfg!(target_env = "msvc") {
            "cl"
        } else {
            "cc"
        }
        .into()
    });
    let mut command = Command::new(&compiler);
    command.current_dir(&out);
    if cfg!(target_env = "msvc") {
        command
            .arg("/nologo")
            .arg("/W4")
            .arg("/WX")
            .arg("/std:c11")
            .arg(format!("/I{}", root.join("include").display()))
            .arg(root.join("crates/codec-ffi/tests/abi.c"))
            .arg(deps.join("codec_ffi.dll.lib"))
            .arg(format!("/Fe:{}", exe.display()));
    } else {
        let library = deps.join(if cfg!(target_os = "macos") {
            "libcodec_ffi.dylib"
        } else if cfg!(windows) {
            "libcodec_ffi.dll.a"
        } else {
            "libcodec_ffi.so"
        });
        command
            .args([
                "-std=c11",
                "-Wall",
                "-Wextra",
                "-Werror",
                "-pedantic-errors",
            ])
            .arg(root.join("crates/codec-ffi/tests/abi.c"))
            .arg("-I")
            .arg(root.join("include"))
            .arg(library)
            .arg("-o")
            .arg(&exe);
        if !cfg!(windows) {
            command.arg(format!("-Wl,-rpath,{}", deps.display()));
        }
    }
    let result = command
        .output()
        .unwrap_or_else(|error| panic!("Required C compiler {compiler} unavailable: {error}"));
    assert!(
        result.status.success(),
        "C compile/link failed: {}",
        String::from_utf8_lossy(&result.stderr)
    );
    let mut run = Command::new(exe);
    for variable in ["PATH", "LD_LIBRARY_PATH", "DYLD_LIBRARY_PATH"] {
        let mut paths = vec![deps.clone()];
        if let Some(value) = env::var_os(variable) {
            paths.extend(env::split_paths(&value));
        }
        run.env(variable, env::join_paths(paths).unwrap());
    }
    assert!(run.status().expect("C ABI executable must run").success());
}

use std::env;
use std::fs;
use std::path::PathBuf;

#[test]
fn test_cbindgen_consistency() {
    let manifest_dir = env::var("CARGO_MANIFEST_DIR").unwrap();
    let manifest_path = PathBuf::from(&manifest_dir);
    let root = manifest_path.parent().unwrap().parent().unwrap();

    let include_dir = root.join("include");
    let existing_header_path = include_dir.join("codec.h");
    let mut generated = Vec::new();

    cbindgen::Builder::new()
        .with_crate(&manifest_dir)
        .with_config(cbindgen::Config::from_file(root.join("cbindgen.toml")).unwrap())
        .generate()
        .expect("Unable to generate bindings")
        .write(&mut generated);

    let generated_header_content = String::from_utf8(generated).expect("UTF-8 bindings");
    let existing_header_content =
        fs::read_to_string(&existing_header_path).expect("checked-in header");

    let normalized_generated = generated_header_content.replace("\r\n", "\n");
    let normalized_existing = existing_header_content.replace("\r\n", "\n");

    if normalized_generated != normalized_existing {
        panic!(
            "The generated C header does not match include/codec.h.\n\
             Please check the differences, and run cbindgen to update include/codec.h."
        );
    }
}

use codec_core::{CodecError, CodecResult, Frame, PixelFormat, Timestamp};
use std::alloc::{alloc_zeroed, dealloc, Layout};
use std::collections::VecDeque;
use std::ptr::NonNull;
use std::sync::atomic::{AtomicU32, Ordering};
use std::sync::Arc;

pub const CACHE_LINE_ALIGN: usize = 64;
pub const SUPERBLOCK_PADDING: usize = 128;
pub const FILTER_HALO_MARGIN: usize = 64;

#[derive(Debug, Clone)]
pub struct Plane {
    pub data: Arc<[u8]>,
    pub stride: usize,
    pub width: usize,
    pub height: usize,
    pub offset: usize,
}

#[derive(Debug, Clone)]
pub struct CPUFrame {
    pub planes: Vec<Plane>,
    pub format: PixelFormat,
    pub width: u32,
    pub height: u32,
    pub pts: Option<Timestamp>,
}

impl Frame for CPUFrame {
    fn width(&self) -> u32 {
        self.width
    }
    fn height(&self) -> u32 {
        self.height
    }
    fn format(&self) -> PixelFormat {
        self.format
    }
    fn pts(&self) -> Option<Timestamp> {
        self.pts
    }
}

impl CPUFrame {
    pub fn new(format: PixelFormat, width: u32, height: u32) -> CodecResult<Self> {
        if width == 0 || height == 0 {
            return Err(CodecError::InvalidData);
        }
        // Bound the aggregate before any plane allocation, including on 32-bit hosts.
        let pixels = u64::from(width) * u64::from(height);
        if pixels > 128 * 1024 * 1024 {
            return Err(CodecError::OutOfMemory);
        }
        let y_stride = width as usize;
        let y_height = height as usize;

        let (planes, _) = match format {
            PixelFormat::Yuv420p => {
                let uv_stride = (width as usize).div_ceil(2);
                let uv_height = (height as usize).div_ceil(2);
                (
                    vec![
                        (y_stride, y_height),
                        (uv_stride, uv_height),
                        (uv_stride, uv_height),
                    ],
                    3,
                )
            }
            PixelFormat::Yuv422p => {
                let uv_stride = (width as usize).div_ceil(2);
                let uv_height = height as usize;
                (
                    vec![
                        (y_stride, y_height),
                        (uv_stride, uv_height),
                        (uv_stride, uv_height),
                    ],
                    3,
                )
            }
            PixelFormat::Yuv444p => {
                let uv_stride = width as usize;
                let uv_height = height as usize;
                (
                    vec![
                        (y_stride, y_height),
                        (uv_stride, uv_height),
                        (uv_stride, uv_height),
                    ],
                    3,
                )
            }
            PixelFormat::Yuv420p10 => {
                let y_stride = (width as usize) * 2;
                let uv_stride = (width as usize).div_ceil(2) * 2;
                let uv_height = (height as usize).div_ceil(2);
                (
                    vec![
                        (y_stride, y_height),
                        (uv_stride, uv_height),
                        (uv_stride, uv_height),
                    ],
                    3,
                )
            }
        };

        let bytes_per_pixel = if format == PixelFormat::Yuv420p10 {
            2
        } else {
            1
        };
        let total = planes
            .iter()
            .try_fold(0usize, |total, &(stride, height)| {
                stride
                    .checked_mul(height)
                    .and_then(|size| total.checked_add(size))
            })
            .ok_or(CodecError::OutOfMemory)?;
        if total > 128 * 1024 * 1024 {
            return Err(CodecError::OutOfMemory);
        }
        let mut plane_data = Vec::new();
        plane_data
            .try_reserve_exact(planes.len())
            .map_err(|_| CodecError::OutOfMemory)?;
        for (stride, h) in planes {
            let data_size = stride
                .checked_mul(h)
                .ok_or(CodecError::OutOfMemory)?;
            // Prevent absurdly large allocations that will OOM
            if data_size > 1024 * 1024 * 128 {
                return Err(CodecError::OutOfMemory);
            }
            let mut bytes = Vec::new();
            bytes
                .try_reserve_exact(data_size)
                .map_err(|_| CodecError::OutOfMemory)?;
            bytes.resize(data_size, 0);
            // Stable Rust has no fallible Arc<[u8]> conversion. Aggregate limits
            // bound this remaining allocation (which can temporarily duplicate bytes).
            let data = Arc::from(bytes.into_boxed_slice());
            plane_data.push(Plane {
                data,
                stride,
                width: stride / bytes_per_pixel,
                height: h,
                offset: 0,
            });
        }

        Ok(Self {
            planes: plane_data,
            format,
            width,
            height,
            pts: None,
        })
    }
}

/// 64-byte cache-aligned, superblock-padded (128x128) plane allocation for SIMD
/// reconstruction, 8-tap motion compensation, CDEF, and loop restoration halos.
pub struct AlignedPlaneBuffer {
    ptr: NonNull<u8>,
    layout: Layout,
    pub visible_width: usize,
    pub visible_height: usize,
    pub padded_width: usize,
    pub padded_height: usize,
    pub stride_bytes: usize,
    pub bytes_per_sample: usize,
    pub origin_offset_bytes: usize,
}

unsafe impl Send for AlignedPlaneBuffer {}
unsafe impl Sync for AlignedPlaneBuffer {}

impl std::fmt::Debug for AlignedPlaneBuffer {
    fn fmt(&self, f: &mut std::fmt::Formatter<'_>) -> std::fmt::Result {
        f.debug_struct("AlignedPlaneBuffer")
            .field("visible_width", &self.visible_width)
            .field("visible_height", &self.visible_height)
            .field("padded_width", &self.padded_width)
            .field("padded_height", &self.padded_height)
            .field("stride_bytes", &self.stride_bytes)
            .field("bytes_per_sample", &self.bytes_per_sample)
            .finish()
    }
}

impl AlignedPlaneBuffer {
    pub fn new_superblock_padded(
        width: usize,
        height: usize,
        bytes_per_sample: usize,
    ) -> CodecResult<Self> {
        if width == 0 || height == 0 || !matches!(bytes_per_sample, 1 | 2) {
            return Err(CodecError::InvalidData);
        }
        let sb_w = width
            .div_ceil(SUPERBLOCK_PADDING)
            .checked_mul(SUPERBLOCK_PADDING)
            .ok_or(CodecError::OutOfMemory)?;
        let sb_h = height
            .div_ceil(SUPERBLOCK_PADDING)
            .checked_mul(SUPERBLOCK_PADDING)
            .ok_or(CodecError::OutOfMemory)?;
        let padded_width = sb_w
            .checked_add(2 * FILTER_HALO_MARGIN)
            .ok_or(CodecError::OutOfMemory)?;
        let padded_height = sb_h
            .checked_add(2 * FILTER_HALO_MARGIN)
            .ok_or(CodecError::OutOfMemory)?;
        let raw_stride = padded_width
            .checked_mul(bytes_per_sample)
            .ok_or(CodecError::OutOfMemory)?;
        let stride_bytes = raw_stride
            .div_ceil(CACHE_LINE_ALIGN)
            .checked_mul(CACHE_LINE_ALIGN)
            .ok_or(CodecError::OutOfMemory)?;
        let total_bytes = stride_bytes
            .checked_mul(padded_height)
            .ok_or(CodecError::OutOfMemory)?;
        if total_bytes > 128 * 1024 * 1024 {
            return Err(CodecError::OutOfMemory);
        }
        let layout =
            Layout::from_size_align(total_bytes, CACHE_LINE_ALIGN).map_err(|_| CodecError::OutOfMemory)?;
        let raw_ptr = unsafe { alloc_zeroed(layout) };
        let ptr = NonNull::new(raw_ptr).ok_or(CodecError::OutOfMemory)?;
        let origin_offset_bytes =
            FILTER_HALO_MARGIN * stride_bytes + FILTER_HALO_MARGIN * bytes_per_sample;
        Ok(Self {
            ptr,
            layout,
            visible_width: width,
            visible_height: height,
            padded_width,
            padded_height,
            stride_bytes,
            bytes_per_sample,
            origin_offset_bytes,
        })
    }

    pub fn as_slice(&self) -> &[u8] {
        unsafe { std::slice::from_raw_parts(self.ptr.as_ptr(), self.layout.size()) }
    }

    pub fn as_mut_slice(&mut self) -> &mut [u8] {
        unsafe { std::slice::from_raw_parts_mut(self.ptr.as_ptr(), self.layout.size()) }
    }

    pub fn zero_fill(&mut self) {
        self.as_mut_slice().fill(0);
    }

    pub fn base_addr(&self) -> usize {
        self.ptr.as_ptr() as usize
    }
}

impl Clone for AlignedPlaneBuffer {
    fn clone(&self) -> Self {
        let raw_ptr = unsafe { alloc_zeroed(self.layout) };
        let ptr = NonNull::new(raw_ptr).expect("AlignedPlaneBuffer clone allocation");
        unsafe {
            std::ptr::copy_nonoverlapping(self.ptr.as_ptr(), ptr.as_ptr(), self.layout.size());
        }
        Self {
            ptr,
            layout: self.layout,
            visible_width: self.visible_width,
            visible_height: self.visible_height,
            padded_width: self.padded_width,
            padded_height: self.padded_height,
            stride_bytes: self.stride_bytes,
            bytes_per_sample: self.bytes_per_sample,
            origin_offset_bytes: self.origin_offset_bytes,
        }
    }
}

impl Drop for AlignedPlaneBuffer {
    fn drop(&mut self) {
        unsafe {
            dealloc(self.ptr.as_ptr(), self.layout);
        }
    }
}

/// Typed storage backing for decoded frames: either 64-byte cache-aligned host
/// planes for software decode or zero-copy Vulkan/DMA-BUF GPU surfaces.
#[derive(Debug, Clone)]
pub enum FrameStorage {
    HostAligned(Arc<AlignedPlaneBuffer>),
    VulkanImage {
        image_view: u64,
        dpb_slot: u8,
        timeline_semaphore: u64,
        timeline_value: u64,
    },
    DmaBuf {
        fd: i32,
        drm_format_modifier: u64,
        offset: usize,
        pitch: usize,
    },
}

/// Physical slot in the Dual-Reference Virtual Buffer Index (VBI) pool.
#[derive(Debug)]
pub struct PhysicalBufferSlot {
    pub slot_index: usize,
    pub decoder_ref_count: AtomicU32,
    pub player_ref_count: AtomicU32,
    pub storage: FrameStorage,
    pub frame_id: u32,
    pub order_hint: u32,
}

impl PhysicalBufferSlot {
    pub fn dec_refs(&self) -> u32 {
        self.decoder_ref_count.load(Ordering::Acquire)
    }

    pub fn player_refs(&self) -> u32 {
        self.player_ref_count.load(Ordering::Acquire)
    }

    pub fn is_free(&self) -> bool {
        self.dec_refs() == 0 && self.player_refs() == 0
    }
}

/// Normative AV1 Dual-Reference Decoder Model (`DecoderRefCount` and `PlayerRefCount`).
/// Decouples reference frame retention (`VBI[0..8]`) from asynchronous display presentation
/// queues and `show_existing_frame` re-emissions.
#[derive(Debug)]
pub struct DualRefBufferPool {
    pub slots: Vec<PhysicalBufferSlot>,
    pub vbi: [Option<usize>; 8],
    pub presentation_queue: VecDeque<usize>,
}

impl DualRefBufferPool {
    /// Creates a pre-allocated pool of `pool_size` slots (must be >= 9, typically 16).
    pub fn new_host_pool(
        pool_size: usize,
        width: usize,
        height: usize,
        bytes_per_sample: usize,
    ) -> CodecResult<Self> {
        if pool_size < 9 {
            return Err(CodecError::InvalidData);
        }
        let mut slots = Vec::with_capacity(pool_size);
        for i in 0..pool_size {
            let buf = AlignedPlaneBuffer::new_superblock_padded(width, height, bytes_per_sample)?;
            slots.push(PhysicalBufferSlot {
                slot_index: i,
                decoder_ref_count: AtomicU32::new(0),
                player_ref_count: AtomicU32::new(0),
                storage: FrameStorage::HostAligned(Arc::new(buf)),
                frame_id: 0,
                order_hint: 0,
            });
        }
        Ok(Self {
            slots,
            vbi: [None; 8],
            presentation_queue: VecDeque::new(),
        })
    }

    /// Selects a free physical buffer slot `b_new` satisfying
    /// `DecoderRefCount[b_new] == 0 && PlayerRefCount[b_new] == 0`.
    pub fn acquire_free_slot(&self) -> CodecResult<usize> {
        self.slots
            .iter()
            .position(PhysicalBufferSlot::is_free)
            .ok_or(CodecError::OutOfMemory)
    }

    /// Atomically updates all 8 virtual slots `i in 0..8` according to `refresh_frame_flags`
    /// and optionally enqueues `b_new` for display presentation if `show_frame` is true.
    pub fn commit_decoded_frame(
        &mut self,
        b_new: usize,
        refresh_frame_flags: u8,
        show_frame: bool,
        frame_id: u32,
        order_hint: u32,
    ) -> CodecResult<()> {
        if b_new >= self.slots.len() {
            return Err(CodecError::InvalidData);
        }
        self.slots[b_new].frame_id = frame_id;
        self.slots[b_new].order_hint = order_hint;

        for i in 0..8 {
            if ((refresh_frame_flags >> i) & 1) == 1 {
                if let Some(old_b) = self.vbi[i] {
                    let prev = self.slots[old_b]
                        .decoder_ref_count
                        .fetch_sub(1, Ordering::AcqRel);
                    debug_assert!(prev >= 1);
                }
                self.vbi[i] = Some(b_new);
                self.slots[b_new]
                    .decoder_ref_count
                    .fetch_add(1, Ordering::AcqRel);
            }
        }

        if show_frame {
            self.slots[b_new]
                .player_ref_count
                .fetch_add(1, Ordering::AcqRel);
            self.presentation_queue.push_back(b_new);
        }
        Ok(())
    }

    /// Handles `show_existing_frame == 1` with `idx = frame_to_show_map_idx`:
    /// No new buffer is allocated; increments `PlayerRefCount[VBI[idx]]` and enqueues
    /// for presentation. If `refresh_all_vbi` is true (e.g. shown frame is KEY_FRAME),
    /// refreshes all 8 VBI slots (`refresh_frame_flags = 255`) to `b_existing`.
    pub fn show_existing_frame(
        &mut self,
        frame_to_show_map_idx: usize,
        refresh_all_vbi: bool,
    ) -> CodecResult<usize> {
        if frame_to_show_map_idx >= 8 {
            return Err(CodecError::InvalidData);
        }
        let b_existing = self.vbi[frame_to_show_map_idx].ok_or(CodecError::InvalidData)?;
        self.slots[b_existing]
            .player_ref_count
            .fetch_add(1, Ordering::AcqRel);
        self.presentation_queue.push_back(b_existing);

        if refresh_all_vbi {
            for i in 0..8 {
                if let Some(old_b) = self.vbi[i] {
                    self.slots[old_b]
                        .decoder_ref_count
                        .fetch_sub(1, Ordering::AcqRel);
                }
                self.vbi[i] = Some(b_existing);
                self.slots[b_existing]
                    .decoder_ref_count
                    .fetch_add(1, Ordering::AcqRel);
            }
        }
        Ok(b_existing)
    }

    /// Pops the next frame from the presentation queue and decrements `PlayerRefCount[b]`.
    pub fn release_presented_frame(&mut self) -> Option<usize> {
        let b = self.presentation_queue.pop_front()?;
        let prev = self.slots[b]
            .player_ref_count
            .fetch_sub(1, Ordering::AcqRel);
        debug_assert!(prev >= 1);
        Some(b)
    }

    /// Clears and zeroes the output buffer on a decoder error without polluting VBI state.
    pub fn rollback_on_error(&mut self, b_current: usize) {
        if let Some(slot) = self.slots.get_mut(b_current) {
            if let FrameStorage::HostAligned(ref mut arc_buf) = slot.storage {
                if let Some(buf) = Arc::get_mut(arc_buf) {
                    buf.zero_fill();
                }
            }
        }
    }

    /// Typestate discontinuous seek reset: zeroes `DecoderRefCount[0..M]` and clears VBI
    /// while leaving `PlayerRefCount[0..M]` and `presentation_queue` intact so the display
    /// renderer never experiences a use-after-free during scrubbing.
    pub fn discontinuous_seek_reset(&mut self) {
        self.vbi = [None; 8];
        for slot in &self.slots {
            slot.decoder_ref_count.store(0, Ordering::Release);
        }
    }
}

#[cfg(test)]
mod tests {
    use super::*;
    #[test]
    fn aggregate_limit_before_allocation() {
        assert!(CPUFrame::new(PixelFormat::Yuv444p, 8192, 8192).is_err());
        assert!(CPUFrame::new(PixelFormat::Yuv420p10, u32::MAX, u32::MAX).is_err());
        assert!(CPUFrame::new(PixelFormat::Yuv420p, 0, 1).is_err());
    }
    #[test]
    fn odd_dimensions_and_shared_planes() {
        let frame = CPUFrame::new(PixelFormat::Yuv420p10, 3, 3).unwrap();
        assert_eq!(frame.planes[0].data.len(), 18);
        assert_eq!(frame.planes[1].data.len(), 8);
        assert_eq!(frame.planes[1].width, 2);
        assert!(Arc::ptr_eq(
            &frame.planes[0].data,
            &frame.clone().planes[0].data
        ));
    }
    #[test]
    fn cache_aligned_superblock_buffer_and_dual_ref_vbi_state_machine() {
        let mut pool = DualRefBufferPool::new_host_pool(16, 320, 240, 1).unwrap();
        if let FrameStorage::HostAligned(ref buf) = pool.slots[0].storage {
            assert_eq!(buf.base_addr() % CACHE_LINE_ALIGN, 0);
            assert_eq!(buf.stride_bytes % CACHE_LINE_ALIGN, 0);
            assert!(buf.padded_width >= 384 + 2 * FILTER_HALO_MARGIN);
        }
        // Keyframe refreshes all 8 VBI slots (255) and is shown
        let b0 = pool.acquire_free_slot().unwrap();
        assert_eq!(b0, 0);
        pool.commit_decoded_frame(b0, 0xff, true, 1, 0).unwrap();
        assert_eq!(pool.slots[b0].dec_refs(), 8);
        assert_eq!(pool.slots[b0].player_refs(), 1);

        // Invisible ALTREF frame refreshes slot 1 only (show_frame = false)
        let b1 = pool.acquire_free_slot().unwrap();
        assert_eq!(b1, 1);
        pool.commit_decoded_frame(b1, 0b0000_0010, false, 2, 4)
            .unwrap();
        assert_eq!(pool.slots[b0].dec_refs(), 7);
        assert_eq!(pool.slots[b1].dec_refs(), 1);
        assert_eq!(pool.slots[b1].player_refs(), 0);

        // show_existing_frame outputs VBI[1] (b1) without allocating a new slot
        let shown = pool.show_existing_frame(1, false).unwrap();
        assert_eq!(shown, b1);
        assert_eq!(pool.slots[b1].player_refs(), 1);

        // Discontinuous seek zeroes DecoderRefCount while preserving PlayerRefCount!
        pool.discontinuous_seek_reset();
        assert_eq!(pool.slots[b0].dec_refs(), 0);
        assert_eq!(pool.slots[b1].dec_refs(), 0);
        assert_eq!(pool.slots[b0].player_refs(), 1);
        assert_eq!(pool.slots[b1].player_refs(), 1);
        assert_eq!(pool.release_presented_frame(), Some(b0));
        assert_eq!(pool.release_presented_frame(), Some(b1));
        assert!(pool.slots[b0].is_free());
        assert!(pool.slots[b1].is_free());
    }
}
